```typescript
// Advanced Form Components
export function AdvancedForm({ children, onFinish, onFinishFailed, loading, ...props }: any) {
  const [form] = Form.useForm();

  const handleSubmit = async (values: any) => {
    try {
      await onFinish(values);
      form.resetFields();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Form submission error:', error);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Form
      form={form}
      layout="vertical"
      onFinish={handleSubmit}
      onFinishFailed={onFinishFailed}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </Form>
  );
}

export function FormField({ 
  name, 
  label, 
  type = 'input', 
  required = false, 
  options = [], 
  placeholder, 
  ...props 
}: {
  name: string;
  label: string;
  type?: 'input' | 'textarea' | 'select' | 'number' | 'date' | 'password';
  required?: boolean;
  options?: { label: string; value: any }[];
  placeholder?: string;
  [key: string]: any;
}) {
  const renderField = () => {
    switch (type) {
      case 'textarea':
        return <Input.TextArea placeholder={placeholder} {...props} />;
      case 'select':
        return (
          <Select placeholder={placeholder} {...props}>
            {options.map(option => (
              <Select.Option key={option.value} value={option.value}>
                {option.label}
              </Select.Option>
            ))}
          </Select>
        );
      case 'number':
        return <InputNumber placeholder={placeholder} {...props} />;
      case 'date':
        return <DatePicker placeholder={placeholder} {...props} />;
      case 'password':
        return <Input.Password placeholder={placeholder} {...props} />;
      default:
        return <Input placeholder={placeholder} {...props} />;
    }
  };

  return (
    <Form.Item
      name={name}
      label={label}
      rules={[
        { required, message: `${label} is required` },
        ...(props.rules || [])
      ]}
    >
      {renderField()}
    </Form.Item>
  );
}

      <p>{content}</p>
    </Modal>
  );
}
```

```typescript
// Loading States and Progress Indicators
export function LoadingOverlay({ loading, children }: { loading: boolean; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
      {children}
      {loading && (
        <div style={{
          position: 'absolute',
          top: 0,
          left: 0,
          right: 0,
          bottom: 0,
          backgroundColor: 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.8)',
          display: 'flex',
          justifyContent: 'center',
          alignItems: 'center',
          zIndex: 1000
        }}>
          <Spin size="large" />
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// Error Boundary Component
export class ErrorBoundary extends React.Component<
  { children: React.ReactNode },
  { hasError: boolean; error: Error | null }
> {
  constructor(props: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
    super(props);
    this.state = { hasError: false, error: null };
  }

  static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error) {
    return { hasError: true, error };
  }

  componentDidCatch(error: Error, errorInfo: React.ErrorInfo) {
    console.error('Error caught by boundary:', error, errorInfo);
  }

  render() {
    if (this.state.hasError) {
      return (
        <div style={{ padding: '50px', textAlign: 'center' }}>
          <h2>Something went wrong</h2>
          <p>{this.state.error?.message}</p>
          <Button onClick={() => window.location.reload()}>
            Reload Page
          </Button>
        </div>
      );
    }

    return this.props.children;
  }
}

// Responsive Layout Component
export function ResponsiveLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [screenSize, setScreenSize] = useState<'xs' | 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl'>('lg');

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleResize = () => {
      const width = window.innerWidth;
      if (width < 576) setScreenSize('xs');
      else if (width < 768) setScreenSize('sm');
      else if (width < 992) setScreenSize('md');
      else if (width < 1200) setScreenSize('lg');
      else setScreenSize('xl');
    };

    handleResize();
    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className={`responsive-layout ${screenSize}`}>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

// Notification System
export function NotificationSystem() {
  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<any[]>([]);

  const addNotification = (notification: any) => {
    const id = Date.now();
    setNotifications(prev => [...prev, { ...notification, id }]);
    
    // Auto remove after 5 seconds
    setTimeout(() => {
      setNotifications(prev => prev.filter(n => n.id !== id));
    }, 5000);
  };

  const removeNotification = (id: number) => {
    setNotifications(prev => prev.filter(n => n.id !== id));
  };

  return (
    <div className="notification-system">
      {notifications.map(notification => (
        <div
          key={notification.id}
          className={`notification ${notification.type}`}
          onClick={() => removeNotification(notification.id)}
        >
          <div className="notification-content">
            <div className="notification-title">{notification.title}</div>
            <div className="notification-message">{notification.message}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
```

Day 12 – Performance Optimization and Caching

Today I focused on optimizing the application's performance and implementing comprehensive caching strategies. The goal was to ensure the system could handle large datasets efficiently while providing fast response times and optimal user experience.

**Morning Session (4 hours):**
I began by analyzing performance bottlenecks and implementing comprehensive caching strategies. The system needed to handle:
- Database query optimization and indexing
- API response caching and memoization
- Frontend component optimization and lazy loading
- Image and asset optimization
- Memory management and garbage collection
- Real-time performance monitoring

I implemented advanced caching mechanisms:

```python
import redis
import json
import hashlib
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Union
from functools import wraps
import asyncio
import time

class AdvancedCacheManager:
    def __init__(self, redis_host='localhost', redis_port=6379, redis_db=0):
        try:
            self.redis_client = redis.Redis(
                host=redis_host, 
                port=redis_port, 
                db=redis_db,
                decode_responses=True,
                socket_connect_timeout=5,
                socket_timeout=5,
                retry_on_timeout=True
            )
            self.redis_client.ping()  # Test connection
            self.connected = True
            logger.info("✅ Redis cache connected successfully")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Redis connection failed: {str(e)}")
            self.connected = False
            self.redis_client = None
    
    def generate_cache_key(self, prefix: str, *args, **kwargs) -> str:
        """Generate a unique cache key"""
        try:
            # Create a hash of all arguments
            key_data = f"{prefix}:{str(args)}:{str(sorted(kwargs.items()))}"
            key_hash = hashlib.md5(key_data.encode()).hexdigest()
            return f"cache:{key_hash}"
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Cache key generation failed: {str(e)}")
            return f"cache:{prefix}:{int(time.time())}"
    
    def set_cache(self, key: str, value: Any, ttl: int = 3600) -> bool:
        """Set cache value with TTL"""
        try:
            if not self.connected:
                return False
            
            # Serialize value
            if isinstance(value, (dict, list)):
                serialized_value = json.dumps(value, default=str)
            else:
                serialized_value = str(value)
            
            # Set with TTL
            result = self.redis_client.setex(key, ttl, serialized_value)
            logger.debug(f"✅ Cache set: {key} (TTL: {ttl}s)")
            return result
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Cache set failed: {str(e)}")
            return False
    
    def get_cache(self, key: str) -> Optional[Any]:
        """Get cache value"""
        try:
            if not self.connected:
                return None
            
            value = self.redis_client.get(key)
            if value is None:
                return None
            
            # Try to deserialize JSON
            try:
                return json.loads(value)
            except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
                return value
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Cache get failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    def delete_cache(self, key: str) -> bool:
        """Delete cache key"""
        try:
            if not self.connected:
                return False
            
            result = self.redis_client.delete(key)
            logger.debug(f"✅ Cache deleted: {key}")
            return result > 0
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Cache delete failed: {str(e)}")
            return False
    
    def clear_pattern(self, pattern: str) -> int:
        """Clear all keys matching pattern"""
        try:
            if not self.connected:
                return 0
            
            keys = self.redis_client.keys(pattern)
            if keys:
                result = self.redis_client.delete(*keys)
                logger.info(f"✅ Cleared {result} keys matching pattern: {pattern}")
                return result
            return 0
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Cache pattern clear failed: {str(e)}")
            return 0
    
    def get_cache_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get cache statistics"""
        try:
            if not self.connected:
                return {"connected": False}
            
            info = self.redis_client.info()
            return {
                "connected": True,
                "used_memory": info.get("used_memory_human"),
                "connected_clients": info.get("connected_clients"),
                "total_commands_processed": info.get("total_commands_processed"),
                "keyspace_hits": info.get("keyspace_hits"),
                "keyspace_misses": info.get("keyspace_misses"),
                "hit_rate": info.get("keyspace_hits") / (info.get("keyspace_hits") + info.get("keyspace_misses")) if (info.get("keyspace_hits") + info.get("keyspace_misses")) > 0 else 0
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Cache stats failed: {str(e)}")
            return {"connected": False, "error": str(e)}

def cache_result(ttl: int = 3600, key_prefix: str = "default"):
    """Decorator for caching function results"""
    def decorator(func):
        @wraps(func)
        async def async_wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
            cache_manager = getattr(func, '_cache_manager', None)
            if not cache_manager:
                cache_manager = AdvancedCacheManager()
                func._cache_manager = cache_manager
            
            # Generate cache key
            cache_key = cache_manager.generate_cache_key(
                key_prefix, 
                func.__name__, 
                *args, 
                **kwargs
            )
            
            # Try to get from cache
            cached_result = cache_manager.get_cache(cache_key)
            if cached_result is not None:
                logger.debug(f"✅ Cache hit: {cache_key}")
                return cached_result
            
            # Execute function and cache result
            try:
                if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(func):
                    result = await func(*args, **kwargs)
                else:
                    result = func(*args, **kwargs)
                
                # Cache result
                cache_manager.set_cache(cache_key, result, ttl)
                logger.debug(f"✅ Cache miss, result cached: {cache_key}")
                return result
                
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"❌ Function execution failed: {str(e)}")
                raise
        
        @wraps(func)
        def sync_wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
            cache_manager = getattr(func, '_cache_manager', None)
            if not cache_manager:
                cache_manager = AdvancedCacheManager()
                func._cache_manager = cache_manager
            
            # Generate cache key
            cache_key = cache_manager.generate_cache_key(
                key_prefix, 
                func.__name__, 
                *args, 
                **kwargs
            )
            
            # Try to get from cache
            cached_result = cache_manager.get_cache(cache_key)
            if cached_result is not None:
                logger.debug(f"✅ Cache hit: {cache_key}")
                return cached_result
            
            # Execute function and cache result
            try:
                result = func(*args, **kwargs)
                
                # Cache result
                cache_manager.set_cache(cache_key, result, ttl)
                logger.debug(f"✅ Cache miss, result cached: {cache_key}")
                return result
                
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error(f"❌ Function execution failed: {str(e)}")
                raise
        
        return async_wrapper if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(func) else sync_wrapper
    return decorator

class DatabaseQueryOptimizer:
    def __init__(self, supabase_client):
        self.supabase = supabase_client
        self.query_cache = {}
        self.index_recommendations = []
    
    def optimize_query(self, query: str, params: Dict = None) -> str:
        """Optimize database query"""
        try:
            # Add query analysis
            optimized_query = self._analyze_and_optimize(query)
            
            # Cache query plan
            query_hash = hashlib.md5(query.encode()).hexdigest()
            self.query_cache[query_hash] = {
                'original': query,
                'optimized': optimized_query,
                'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat()
            }
            
            return optimized_query
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Query optimization failed: {str(e)}")
            return query
    
    def _analyze_and_optimize(self, query: str) -> str:
        """Analyze and optimize SQL query"""
        try:
            # Basic optimizations
            optimized = query.strip()
            
            # Add LIMIT if missing for large result sets
            if 'SELECT' in optimized.upper() and 'LIMIT' not in optimized.upper():
                if 'ORDER BY' in optimized.upper():
                    optimized += ' LIMIT 1000'
            
            # Add index recommendations
            self._analyze_indexes(optimized)
            
            return optimized
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Query analysis failed: {str(e)}")
            return query
    
    def _analyze_indexes(self, query: str):
        """Analyze query for index recommendations"""
        try:
            # Simple index analysis
            if 'WHERE' in query.upper():
                # Extract WHERE conditions
                where_start = query.upper().find('WHERE')
                if where_start != -1:
                    where_clause = query[where_start + 5:].split('ORDER BY')[0].split('GROUP BY')[0]
                    
                    # Recommend indexes for common patterns
                    if 'username' in where_clause.lower():
                        self.index_recommendations.append({
                            'table': 'leads',
                            'column': 'username',
                            'type': 'btree',
                            'reason': 'Username lookups are frequent'
                        })
                    
                    if 'nationality' in where_clause.lower():
                        self.index_recommendations.append({
                            'table': 'leads',
                            'column': 'nationality',
                            'type': 'btree',
                            'reason': 'Nationality filtering is common'
                        })
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Index analysis failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def get_index_recommendations(self) -> List[Dict]:
        """Get index recommendations"""
        return self.index_recommendations
    
    def create_recommended_indexes(self) -> bool:
        """Create recommended indexes"""
        try:
            for recommendation in self.index_recommendations:
                index_sql = f"""
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_{recommendation['table']}_{recommendation['column']} 
                ON {recommendation['table']} ({recommendation['column']})
                """
                
                try:
                    self.supabase.rpc('exec_sql', {'sql': index_sql}).execute()
                    logger.info(f"✅ Created index: {recommendation['column']} on {recommendation['table']}")
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.warning(f"⚠️ Index creation failed: {str(e)}")
            
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Index creation failed: {str(e)}")
            return False
```

```typescript
import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo, useCallback, lazy, Suspense } from 'react';
import { Spin, Alert } from 'antd';

// Lazy loading components
const LeadsDataTable = lazy(() => import('./LeadsDataTable'));
const SessionsDataTable = lazy(() => import('./SessionsDataTable'));
const CampaignDashboard = lazy(() => import('./CampaignDashboard'));

// Performance monitoring hook
export function usePerformanceMonitor(componentName: string) {
  const [metrics, setMetrics] = useState({
    renderTime: 0,
    mountTime: 0,
    updateCount: 0
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    const startTime = performance.now();
    
    return () => {
      const endTime = performance.now();
      const renderTime = endTime - startTime;
      
      setMetrics(prev => ({
        ...prev,
        renderTime,
        updateCount: prev.updateCount + 1
      }));
      
      // Log performance metrics
      if (renderTime > 100) { // Log slow renders
        console.warn(`Slow render in ${componentName}: ${renderTime}ms`);
      }
    };
  });

  return metrics;
}

// Memoized components for performance
export const MemoizedLeadsTable = React.memo(LeadsDataTable, (prevProps, nextProps) => {
  // Custom comparison for re-rendering
  return (
    prevProps.data === nextProps.data &&
    prevProps.loading === nextProps.loading &&
    prevProps.selectedRows === nextProps.selectedRows
  );
});

export const MemoizedSessionsTable = React.memo(SessionsDataTable, (prevProps, nextProps) => {

  );

// Virtual scrolling for large datasets
export function VirtualizedTable({ 
  data, 
  columns, 
  height = 400, 
  itemHeight = 50 
}: {
  data: any[];
  columns: any[];
  height?: number;
  itemHeight?: number;
}) {
  const [scrollTop, setScrollTop] = useState(0);
  const [containerHeight, setContainerHeight] = useState(height);
  
  const visibleCount = Math.ceil(containerHeight / itemHeight);
  const startIndex = Math.floor(scrollTop / itemHeight);
  const endIndex = Math.min(startIndex + visibleCount + 1, data.length);
  
  const visibleData = useMemo(() => {
    return data.slice(startIndex, endIndex);
  }, [data, startIndex, endIndex]);
  
  const totalHeight = data.length * itemHeight;
  
  const handleScroll = useCallback((e: React.UIEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
    setScrollTop(e.currentTarget.scrollTop);
  }, []);
  
  return (
    <div 
      style={{ height: containerHeight, overflow: 'auto' }}
      onScroll={handleScroll}
    >
      <div style={{ height: totalHeight, position: 'relative' }}>
        <div 
          style={{ 
            transform: `translateY(${startIndex * itemHeight}px)`,
            position: 'absolute',
            top: 0,
            left: 0,
            right: 0
          }}
        >
          {visibleData.map((item, index) => (
            <div 
              key={item.id || index}
              style={{ 
                height: itemHeight,
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                padding: '0 16px',
                borderBottom: '1px solid #f0f0f0'
              }}
            >
              {/* Render row content */}
              {columns.map(column => (
                <div key={column.key} style={{ flex: 1 }}>
                  {column.render ? column.render(item[column.dataIndex], item, index) : item[column.dataIndex]}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// Image optimization component
export function OptimizedImage({ 
  src, 
  alt, 
  width, 
  height, 
  placeholder = true 
}: {
  src: string;
  alt: string;
  width?: number;
  height?: number;
  placeholder?: boolean;
}) {
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState(false);
  
  const handleLoad = useCallback(() => {
    setLoaded(true);
  }, []);
  
  const handleError = useCallback(() => {
    setError(true);
  }, []);
  
  return (
    <div style={{ position: 'relative', width, height }}>
      {placeholder && !loaded && !error && (
        <div 
          style={{ 
            position: 'absolute',
            top: 0,
            left: 0,
            right: 0,
            bottom: 0,
            backgroundColor: '#f0f0f0',
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'center'
          }}
        >
          <Spin size="small" />
        </div>
      )}
      
      <img
        src={src}
        alt={alt}
        width={width}
        height={height}
        onLoad={handleLoad}
        onError={handleError}
        style={{ 
          display: loaded && !error ? 'block' : 'none',
          width: '100%',
          height: '100%',
          objectFit: 'cover'
        }}
      />
      
      {error && (
        <div 
          style={{ 
            position: 'absolute',
            top: 0,
            left: 0,
            right: 0,
            bottom: 0,
            backgroundColor: '#f0f0f0',
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'center',
            color: '#999'
          }}
        >
          Image failed to load
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

// Debounced search hook
export function useDebounce<T>(value: T, delay: number): T {
  const [debouncedValue, setDebouncedValue] = useState<T>(value);
  
  useEffect(() => {
    const handler = setTimeout(() => {
      setDebouncedValue(value);
    }, delay);
    
    return () => {
      clearTimeout(handler);
    };
  }, [value, delay]);
  
  return debouncedValue;
}

// Performance-optimized search
export function useOptimizedSearch<T>(
  data: T[],
  searchTerm: string,
  searchFields: string[],
  debounceDelay: number = 300
) {
  const [searchResults, setSearchResults] = useState<T[]>(data);
  const debouncedSearchTerm = useDebounce(searchTerm, debounceDelay);
  
  const filteredData = useMemo(() => {
    if (!debouncedSearchTerm.trim()) {
      return data;
    }
    
    const term = debouncedSearchTerm.toLowerCase();
    
    return data.filter(item => {
      return searchFields.some(field => {
        const value = (item as any)[field];
        if (typeof value === 'string') {
          return value.toLowerCase().includes(term);
        }
        if (typeof value === 'number') {
          return value.toString().includes(term);
        }
        return false;
      });
    });
  }, [data, debouncedSearchTerm, searchFields]);
  
  useEffect(() => {
    setSearchResults(filteredData);
  }, [filteredData]);
  
  return {
    searchResults,
    isSearching: searchTerm !== debouncedSearchTerm
  };
}

// Memory management hook
export function useMemoryManagement() {
  const [memoryUsage, setMemoryUsage] = useState<{
    used: number;
    total: number;
    percentage: number;
  }>({ used: 0, total: 0, percentage: 0 });
  
  useEffect(() => {
    const updateMemoryUsage = () => {
      if ('memory' in performance) {
        const memory = (performance as any).memory;
        const used = memory.usedJSHeapSize;
        const total = memory.totalJSHeapSize;
        const percentage = (used / total) * 100;
        
        setMemoryUsage({ used, total, percentage });
        
        // Warn if memory usage is high
        if (percentage > 80) {
          console.warn(`High memory usage: ${percentage.toFixed(2)}%`);
        }
      }
    };
    
    updateMemoryUsage();
    const interval = setInterval(updateMemoryUsage, 5000);
    
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);
  
  return memoryUsage;
}

// Lazy loading wrapper
export function LazyWrapper({ 
  children, 
  fallback = <Spin size="large" /> 
}: { 
  children: React.ReactNode; 
  fallback?: React.ReactNode; 
}) {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={fallback}>
      {children}
    </Suspense>
  );
}

// Performance monitoring component
export function PerformanceMonitor() {
  const [metrics, setMetrics] = useState<any[]>([]);
  
  useEffect(() => {
    const observer = new PerformanceObserver((list) => {
      const entries = list.getEntries();
      entries.forEach(entry => {
        if (entry.entryType === 'measure') {
          setMetrics(prev => [...prev, {
            name: entry.name,
            duration: entry.duration,
            timestamp: Date.now()
          }]);
        }
      });
    });
    
    observer.observe({ entryTypes: ['measure'] });
    
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, []);
  
  return (
    <div style={{ position: 'fixed', top: 10, right: 10, zIndex: 1000 }}>
      <div style={{ backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.8)', color: 'white', padding: '8px', borderRadius: '4px' }}>
        <div>Performance Metrics:</div>
        {metrics.slice(-5).map((metric, index) => (
          <div key={index} style={{ fontSize: '12px' }}>
            {metric.name}: {metric.duration.toFixed(2)}ms
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

**Advanced Caching Strategies:**
I implemented sophisticated caching mechanisms:

```python
class IntelligentCacheManager:
    def __init__(self, redis_client, supabase_client):
        self.redis = redis_client
        self.supabase = supabase_client
        self.cache_hit_rates = {}
        self.cache_patterns = {}
        self.auto_invalidation = True
    
    def smart_cache(self, key_prefix: str, ttl: int = 3600, 
                   auto_refresh: bool = True, 
                   refresh_threshold: float = 0.1):
        """Smart caching with auto-refresh and invalidation"""
        def decorator(func):
            @wraps(func)
            async def async_wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
                cache_key = self._generate_smart_key(key_prefix, func.__name__, *args, **kwargs)
                
                # Check cache
                cached_data = self.redis.get_cache(cache_key)
                if cached_data:
                    self._update_hit_rate(cache_key)
                    
                    # Auto-refresh if needed
                    if auto_refresh and self._should_refresh(cache_key, refresh_threshold):
                        asyncio.create_task(self._refresh_cache_async(func, cache_key, *args, **kwargs))
                    
                    return cached_data
                
                # Cache miss - execute and cache
                try:
                    if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(func):
                        result = await func(*args, **kwargs)
                    else:
                        result = func(*args, **kwargs)
                    
                    self.redis.set_cache(cache_key, result, ttl)
                    self._update_miss_rate(cache_key)
                    return result
                    
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.error(f"❌ Smart cache execution failed: {str(e)}")
                    raise
            
            return async_wrapper
        return decorator
    
    def _generate_smart_key(self, prefix: str, func_name: str, *args, **kwargs) -> str:
        """Generate smart cache key with context awareness"""
        try:
            # Include relevant context in key
            context = {
                'function': func_name,
                'args': str(args),
                'kwargs': str(sorted(kwargs.items())),
                'timestamp': int(time.time() // 300)  # 5-minute buckets
            }
            
            key_data = f"{prefix}:{json.dumps(context, sort_keys=True)}"
            return hashlib.md5(key_data.encode()).hexdigest()
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Smart key generation failed: {str(e)}")
            return f"{prefix}:{func_name}:{int(time.time())}"
    
    def _should_refresh(self, cache_key: str, threshold: float) -> bool:
        """Determine if cache should be refreshed"""
        try:
            hit_rate = self.cache_hit_rates.get(cache_key, 0)
            return hit_rate < threshold
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Refresh decision failed: {str(e)}")
            return False
    
    async def _refresh_cache_async(self, func, cache_key: str, *args, **kwargs):
        """Asynchronously refresh cache"""
        try:
            if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(func):
                result = await func(*args, **kwargs)
            else:
                result = func(*args, **kwargs)
            
            self.redis.set_cache(cache_key, result, 3600)
            logger.info(f"✅ Cache refreshed: {cache_key}")
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Cache refresh failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def invalidate_pattern(self, pattern: str) -> int:
        """Invalidate cache keys matching pattern"""
        try:
            return self.redis.clear_pattern(pattern)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Pattern invalidation failed: {str(e)}")
            return 0
    
    def get_cache_analytics(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get comprehensive cache analytics"""
        try:
            stats = self.redis.get_cache_stats()
            hit_rates = self.cache_hit_rates
            
            return {
                'redis_stats': stats,
                'hit_rates': hit_rates,
                'total_keys': len(hit_rates),
                'avg_hit_rate': sum(hit_rates.values()) / len(hit_rates) if hit_rates else 0,
                'recommendations': self._generate_recommendations()
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Cache analytics failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    def _generate_recommendations(self) -> List[str]:
        """Generate cache optimization recommendations"""
        recommendations = []
        
        try:
            # Analyze hit rates
            low_hit_rate_keys = [k for k, v in self.cache_hit_rates.items() if v < 0.3]
            if low_hit_rate_keys:
                recommendations.append(f"Consider removing {len(low_hit_rate_keys)} low-hit-rate keys")
            
            # Analyze patterns
            if len(self.cache_hit_rates) > 1000:
                recommendations.append("Consider implementing cache eviction policy")
            
            return recommendations
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Recommendation generation failed: {str(e)}")
            return []
```

**Morning Session (4 hours):**
I began by analyzing the error handling requirements and designing a comprehensive logging architecture. The system needed to handle:
- Structured logging with different severity levels
- Error tracking and monitoring
- Performance metrics and analytics
- User activity logging
- System health monitoring
- Alert notifications and reporting

I implemented an advanced logging system:

```python
import logging
import json
import traceback
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from enum import Enum
import asyncio
import threading
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
import uuid

class LogLevel(Enum):
    DEBUG = "DEBUG"
    INFO = "INFO"
    WARNING = "WARNING"
    ERROR = "ERROR"
    CRITICAL = "CRITICAL"

class LogCategory(Enum):
    SYSTEM = "SYSTEM"
    USER_ACTION = "USER_ACTION"
    API_REQUEST = "API_REQUEST"
    DATABASE = "DATABASE"
    SCRAPING = "SCRAPING"
    CLASSIFICATION = "CLASSIFICATION"
    CAMPAIGN = "CAMPAIGN"
    PERFORMANCE = "PERFORMANCE"
    SECURITY = "SECURITY"

@dataclass
class LogEntry:
    id: str
    timestamp: str
    level: LogLevel
    category: LogCategory
    message: str
    details: Dict[str, Any]
    user_id: Optional[str]
    session_id: Optional[str]
    request_id: Optional[str]
    error_code: Optional[str]
    stack_trace: Optional[str]
    metadata: Dict[str, Any]

class AdvancedLogger:
    def __init__(self, name: str, supabase_client=None, redis_client=None):
        self.name = name
        self.supabase = supabase_client
        self.redis = redis_client
        self.log_entries = []
        self.error_counts = {}
        self.performance_metrics = {}
        
        # Setup logging configuration
        self._setup_logging()
        
        # Initialize log processors
        self._initialize_processors()
    
    def _setup_logging(self):
        """Setup logging configuration"""
        try:
            # Create logger
            self.logger = logging.getLogger(self.name)
            self.logger.setLevel(logging.DEBUG)
            
            # Create formatters
            detailed_formatter = logging.Formatter(
                '%(asctime)s - %(name)s - %(levelname)s - %(category)s - %(message)s'
            )
            
            simple_formatter = logging.Formatter(
                '%(asctime)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s'
            )
            
            # Console handler
            console_handler = logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout)
            console_handler.setLevel(logging.INFO)
            console_handler.setFormatter(simple_formatter)
            
            # File handler
            file_handler = logging.FileHandler('application.log')
            file_handler.setLevel(logging.DEBUG)
            file_handler.setFormatter(detailed_formatter)
            
            # Error file handler
            error_handler = logging.FileHandler('errors.log')
            error_handler.setLevel(logging.ERROR)
            error_handler.setFormatter(detailed_formatter)
            
            # Add handlers
            self.logger.addHandler(console_handler)
            self.logger.addHandler(file_handler)
            self.logger.addHandler(error_handler)
            
            logger.info("✅ Advanced logger initialized successfully")
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"❌ Logger setup failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _initialize_processors(self):
        """Initialize log processors"""
        try:
            self.processors = {
                'database': self._process_database_log,
                'performance': self._process_performance_log,
                'error': self._process_error_log,
                'security': self._process_security_log
            }
            
            # Start background processors
            self._start_background_processors()
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Log processors initialization failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _start_background_processors(self):
        """Start background log processors"""
        try:
            # Start log processing thread
            processing_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._process_logs_background)
            processing_thread.daemon = True
            processing_thread.start()
            
            # Start metrics collection thread
            metrics_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._collect_metrics_background)
            metrics_thread.daemon = True
            metrics_thread.start()
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Background processors failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def log(self, level: LogLevel, category: LogCategory, message: str, 
            details: Dict[str, Any] = None, user_id: str = None, 
            session_id: str = None, request_id: str = None,
            error_code: str = None, exception: Exception = None):
        """Log an entry with comprehensive details"""
        try:
            # Generate log entry
            log_entry = LogEntry(
                id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
                timestamp=datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
                level=level,
                category=category,
                message=message,
                details=details or {},
                user_id=user_id,
                session_id=session_id,
                request_id=request_id,
                error_code=error_code,
                stack_trace=traceback.format_exc() if exception else None,
                metadata={
                    'logger_name': self.name,
                    'python_version': sys.version,
                    'platform': sys.platform
                }
            )
            
            # Add to local storage
            self.log_entries.append(log_entry)
            
            # Process log entry
            self._process_log_entry(log_entry)
            
            # Update error counts
            if level in [LogLevel.ERROR, LogLevel.CRITICAL]:
                self._update_error_counts(log_entry)
            
            # Log to standard logger
            self._log_to_standard(log_entry)
            
            # Store in database if available
            if self.supabase:
                asyncio.create_task(self._store_log_async(log_entry))
            
            # Cache recent logs
            if self.redis:
                self._cache_recent_log(log_entry)
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"❌ Logging failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _process_log_entry(self, log_entry: LogEntry):
        """Process log entry"""
        try:
            # Get processor for category
            processor = self.processors.get(log_entry.category.value.lower())
            if processor:
                processor(log_entry)
            
            # Update performance metrics
            if log_entry.category == LogCategory.PERFORMANCE:
                self._update_performance_metrics(log_entry)
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Log processing failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _process_database_log(self, log_entry: LogEntry):
        """Process database-related logs"""
        try:
            if 'query_time' in log_entry.details:
                query_time = log_entry.details['query_time']
                if query_time > 1.0:  # Slow query
                    self.log(LogLevel.WARNING, LogCategory.PERFORMANCE, 
                           f"Slow database query detected: {query_time}s",
                           {'query': log_entry.details.get('query', 'Unknown')})
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Database log processing failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _process_performance_log(self, log_entry: LogEntry):
        """Process performance-related logs"""
        try:
            if 'execution_time' in log_entry.details:
                execution_time = log_entry.details['execution_time']
                self.performance_metrics[log_entry.id] = {
                    'timestamp': log_entry.timestamp,
                    'execution_time': execution_time,
                    'category': log_entry.category.value
                }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Performance log processing failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _process_error_log(self, log_entry: LogEntry):
        """Process error-related logs"""
        try:
            # Categorize error
            error_type = self._categorize_error(log_entry)
            
            # Update error statistics
            self.error_counts[error_type] = self.error_counts.get(error_type, 0) + 1
            
            # Check for error patterns
            self._check_error_patterns(log_entry)
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Error log processing failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _process_security_log(self, log_entry: LogEntry):
        """Process security-related logs"""
        try:
            # Check for security threats
            if 'failed_login' in log_entry.details:
                self._check_brute_force_attempts(log_entry)
            
            if 'suspicious_activity' in log_entry.details:
                self._alert_security_team(log_entry)
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Security log processing failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _categorize_error(self, log_entry: LogEntry) -> str:
        """Categorize error type"""
        try:
            message = log_entry.message.lower()
            
            if 'connection' in message or 'timeout' in message:
                return 'CONNECTION_ERROR'
            elif 'permission' in message or 'access' in message:
                return 'PERMISSION_ERROR'
            elif 'validation' in message or 'invalid' in message:
                return 'VALIDATION_ERROR'
            elif 'not found' in message:
                return 'NOT_FOUND_ERROR'
            else:
                return 'GENERAL_ERROR'
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Error categorization failed: {str(e)}")
            return 'UNKNOWN_ERROR'
    
    def _check_error_patterns(self, log_entry: LogEntry):
        """Check for error patterns and trends"""
        try:
            # Check for repeated errors
            recent_errors = [entry for entry in self.log_entries[-10:] 
                           if entry.level in [LogLevel.ERROR, LogLevel.CRITICAL]]
            
            if len(recent_errors) >= 5:
                self.log(LogLevel.WARNING, LogCategory.SYSTEM,
                        "High error rate detected",
                        {'error_count': len(recent_errors)})
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Error pattern checking failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _check_brute_force_attempts(self, log_entry: LogEntry):
        """Check for brute force login attempts"""
        try:
            user_id = log_entry.user_id
            if not user_id:
                return
            
            # Count recent failed attempts
            recent_failures = [entry for entry in self.log_entries[-20:]
                              if entry.user_id == user_id and 
                              'failed_login' in entry.details]
            
            if len(recent_failures) >= 3:
                self.log(LogLevel.CRITICAL, LogCategory.SECURITY,
                        "Potential brute force attack detected",
                        {'user_id': user_id, 'attempts': len(recent_failures)})
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Brute force checking failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _alert_security_team(self, log_entry: LogEntry):
        """Alert security team about suspicious activity"""
        try:
            # In a real implementation, this would send alerts
            self.log(LogLevel.CRITICAL, LogCategory.SECURITY,
                    "Security alert triggered",
                    {'original_log': log_entry.message})
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Security alert failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def _store_log_async(self, log_entry: LogEntry):
        """Store log entry in database asynchronously"""
        try:
            if not self.supabase:
                return
            
            # Convert to dict for storage
            log_data = asdict(log_entry)
            
            # Store in database
            result = self.supabase.table('application_logs').insert(log_data).execute()
            
            if result.data:
                logger.debug(f"✅ Log stored in database: {log_entry.id}")
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Log storage failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _cache_recent_log(self, log_entry: LogEntry):
        """Cache recent log entry in Redis"""
        try:
            if not self.redis:
                return
            
            # Cache recent logs (last 100)
            cache_key = f"recent_logs:{self.name}"
            self.redis.lpush(cache_key, json.dumps(asdict(log_entry)))
            self.redis.ltrim(cache_key, 0, 99)  # Keep only last 100
            self.redis.expire(cache_key, 3600)  # Expire in 1 hour
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Log caching failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _log_to_standard(self, log_entry: LogEntry):
        """Log to standard Python logger"""
        try:
            # Format message
            formatted_message = f"[{log_entry.category.value}] {log_entry.message}"
            
            # Add details if present
            if log_entry.details:
                formatted_message += f" | Details: {json.dumps(log_entry.details)}"
            
            # Log with appropriate level
            if log_entry.level == LogLevel.DEBUG:
                self.logger.debug(formatted_message)
            elif log_entry.level == LogLevel.INFO:
                self.logger.info(formatted_message)
            elif log_entry.level == LogLevel.WARNING:
                self.logger.warning(formatted_message)
            elif log_entry.level == LogLevel.ERROR:
                self.logger.error(formatted_message)
            elif log_entry.level == LogLevel.CRITICAL:
                self.logger.critical(formatted_message)
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"❌ Standard logging failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _process_logs_background(self):
        """Background log processing"""
        try:
            while True:
                # Process pending logs
                if self.log_entries:
                    # Analyze log patterns
                    self._analyze_log_patterns()
                    
                    # Clean up old logs
                    self._cleanup_old_logs()
                
                # Sleep for 30 seconds
                threading.Event().wait(30)
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Background log processing failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _collect_metrics_background(self):
        """Background metrics collection"""
        try:
            while True:
                # Collect system metrics
                self._collect_system_metrics()
                
                # Update performance statistics
                self._update_performance_statistics()
                
                # Sleep for 60 seconds
                threading.Event().wait(60)
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Background metrics collection failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _analyze_log_patterns(self):
        """Analyze log patterns for insights"""
        try:
            # Analyze error trends
            error_trends = self._analyze_error_trends()
            
            # Analyze performance trends
            performance_trends = self._analyze_performance_trends()
            
            # Generate insights
            insights = {
                'error_trends': error_trends,
                'performance_trends': performance_trends,
                'recommendations': self._generate_recommendations()
            }
            
            # Log insights
            self.log(LogLevel.INFO, LogCategory.SYSTEM,
                    "Log analysis completed",
                    {'insights': insights})
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Log pattern analysis failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _analyze_error_trends(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Analyze error trends"""
        try:
            # Get recent errors
            recent_errors = [entry for entry in self.log_entries[-100:]
                           if entry.level in [LogLevel.ERROR, LogLevel.CRITICAL]]
            
            # Count by category
            error_counts = {}
            for error in recent_errors:
                category = error.category.value
                error_counts[category] = error_counts.get(category, 0) + 1
            
            return {
                'total_errors': len(recent_errors),
                'error_by_category': error_counts,
                'trend': 'increasing' if len(recent_errors) > 50 else 'stable'
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Error trend analysis failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    def _analyze_performance_trends(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Analyze performance trends"""
        try:
            # Get performance metrics
            performance_entries = [entry for entry in self.log_entries[-50:]
                                  if entry.category == LogCategory.PERFORMANCE]
            
            if not performance_entries:
                return {}
            
            # Calculate average execution time
            execution_times = [entry.details.get('execution_time', 0) 
                              for entry in performance_entries]
            avg_execution_time = sum(execution_times) / len(execution_times)
            
            return {
                'avg_execution_time': avg_execution_time,
                'performance_entries': len(performance_entries),
                'trend': 'improving' if avg_execution_time < 1.0 else 'degrading'
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Performance trend analysis failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    def _generate_recommendations(self) -> List[str]:
        """Generate system recommendations"""
        try:
            recommendations = []
            
            # Check error rates
            if self.error_counts.get('CONNECTION_ERROR', 0) > 10:
                recommendations.append("Consider implementing connection pooling")
            
            if self.error_counts.get('PERMISSION_ERROR', 0) > 5:
                recommendations.append("Review user permissions and access controls")
            
            # Check performance
            if self.performance_metrics:
                avg_time = sum(metrics['execution_time'] for metrics in self.performance_metrics.values()) / len(self.performance_metrics)
                if avg_time > 2.0:
                    recommendations.append("Consider optimizing slow operations")
            
            return recommendations
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Recommendation generation failed: {str(e)}")
            return []
    
    def get_log_statistics(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get comprehensive log statistics"""
        try:
            total_logs = len(self.log_entries)
            error_logs = len([entry for entry in self.log_entries 
                             if entry.level in [LogLevel.ERROR, LogLevel.CRITICAL]])
            
            return {
                'total_logs': total_logs,
                'error_logs': error_logs,
                'error_rate': (error_logs / total_logs * 100) if total_logs > 0 else 0,
                'error_counts': self.error_counts,
                'performance_metrics': len(self.performance_metrics),
                'recommendations': self._generate_recommendations()
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Log statistics failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
```

**Afternoon Session (4 hours):**
I implemented comprehensive error handling and monitoring:

```python
class ErrorHandler:
    def __init__(self, logger: AdvancedLogger):
        self.logger = logger
        self.error_patterns = {}
        self.recovery_strategies = {}
        self.alert_thresholds = {}
    
    def handle_error(self, error: Exception, context: Dict[str, Any] = None):
        """Handle errors with comprehensive logging and recovery"""
        try:
            # Log error details
            self.logger.log(
                LogLevel.ERROR,
                LogCategory.SYSTEM,
                f"Error occurred: {str(error)}",
                details=context or {},
                error_code=type(error).__name__,
                exception=error
            )
            
            # Determine recovery strategy
            recovery_strategy = self._determine_recovery_strategy(error)
            
            # Execute recovery
            if recovery_strategy:
                self._execute_recovery(recovery_strategy, error, context)
            
            # Check for error patterns
            self._check_error_patterns(error)
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"❌ Error handling failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _determine_recovery_strategy(self, error: Exception) -> Optional[str]:
        """Determine appropriate recovery strategy for error"""
        try:
            error_type = type(error).__name__
            
            if error_type == 'ConnectionError':
                return 'retry_with_backoff'
            elif error_type == 'TimeoutError':
                return 'retry_with_timeout'
            elif error_type == 'PermissionError':
                return 'escalate_to_admin'
            elif error_type == 'ValidationError':
                return 'return_validation_error'
            else:
                return 'log_and_continue'
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Recovery strategy determination failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    def _execute_recovery(self, strategy: str, error: Exception, context: Dict[str, Any]):
        """Execute recovery strategy"""
        try:
            if strategy == 'retry_with_backoff':
                self._retry_with_backoff(error, context)
            elif strategy == 'retry_with_timeout':
                self._retry_with_timeout(error, context)
            elif strategy == 'escalate_to_admin':
                self._escalate_to_admin(error, context)
            elif strategy == 'return_validation_error':
                self._return_validation_error(error, context)
            elif strategy == 'log_and_continue':
                self._log_and_continue(error, context)
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Recovery execution failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _retry_with_backoff(self, error: Exception, context: Dict[str, Any]):
        """Retry operation with exponential backoff"""
        try:
            max_retries = context.get('max_retries', 3)
            base_delay = context.get('base_delay', 1)
            
            for attempt in range(max_retries):
                try:
                    # Wait with exponential backoff
                    delay = base_delay * (2 ** attempt)
                    time.sleep(delay)
                    
                    # Retry the operation
                    if 'retry_function' in context:
                        context['retry_function']()
                        break
                    
                except Exception as retry_error:
                    if attempt == max_retries - 1:
                        self.logger.log(
                            LogLevel.ERROR,
                            LogCategory.SYSTEM,
                            f"Retry failed after {max_retries} attempts",
                            {'original_error': str(error), 'retry_error': str(retry_error)}
                        )
                    continue
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Retry with backoff failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _escalate_to_admin(self, error: Exception, context: Dict[str, Any]):
        """Escalate error to administrator"""
        try:
            self.logger.log(
                LogLevel.CRITICAL,
                LogCategory.SYSTEM,
                "Error escalated to administrator",
                {
                    'error': str(error),
                    'context': context,
                    'escalation_time': datetime.now().isoformat()
                }
            )
            
            # In a real implementation, this would send notifications
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Error escalation failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _check_error_patterns(self, error: Exception):
        """Check for error patterns and trends"""
        try:
            error_type = type(error).__name__
            current_time = datetime.now()
            
            # Update error patterns
            if error_type not in self.error_patterns:
                self.error_patterns[error_type] = []
            
            self.error_patterns[error_type].append(current_time)
            
            # Clean old patterns (keep last 24 hours)
            cutoff_time = current_time - timedelta(hours=24)
            self.error_patterns[error_type] = [
                timestamp for timestamp in self.error_patterns[error_type]
                if timestamp > cutoff_time
            ]
            
            # Check for error spikes
            if len(self.error_patterns[error_type]) > 10:
                self.logger.log(
                    LogLevel.WARNING,
                    LogCategory.SYSTEM,
                    f"High error rate detected for {error_type}",
                    {'error_count': len(self.error_patterns[error_type])}
                )
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Error pattern checking failed: {str(e)}")

class SystemMonitor:
    def __init__(self, logger: AdvancedLogger):
        self.logger = logger
        self.metrics = {}
        self.alerts = []
        self.health_checks = {}
    
    def monitor_system_health(self):
        """Monitor overall system health"""
        try:
            # Check database connectivity
            self._check_database_health()
            
            # Check Redis connectivity
            self._check_redis_health()
            
            # Check API endpoints
            self._check_api_health()
            
            # Check disk space
            self._check_disk_space()
            
            # Check memory usage
            self._check_memory_usage()
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ System health monitoring failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _check_database_health(self):
        """Check database connectivity and performance"""
        try:
            start_time = time.time()
            
            # Test database connection
            # This would be a real database query in practice
            result = {"status": "healthy", "response_time": 0.1}
            
            execution_time = time.time() - start_time
            
            if execution_time > 1.0:
                self.logger.log(
                    LogLevel.WARNING,
                    LogCategory.PERFORMANCE,
                    "Slow database response detected",
                    {'execution_time': execution_time}
                )
            
            self.logger.log(
                LogLevel.INFO,
                LogCategory.DATABASE,
                "Database health check completed",
                {'execution_time': execution_time, 'status': result['status']}
            )
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.log(
                LogLevel.ERROR,
                LogCategory.DATABASE,
                "Database health check failed",
                {'error': str(e)}
            )
    
    def _check_redis_health(self):
        """Check Redis connectivity and performance"""
        try:
            start_time = time.time()
            
            # Test Redis connection
            # This would be a real Redis operation in practice
            result = {"status": "healthy", "response_time": 0.05}
            
            execution_time = time.time() - start_time
            
            self.logger.log(
                LogLevel.INFO,
                LogCategory.DATABASE,
                "Redis health check completed",
                {'execution_time': execution_time, 'status': result['status']}
            )
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.log(
                LogLevel.ERROR,
                LogCategory.DATABASE,
                "Redis health check failed",
                {'error': str(e)}
            )
    
    def _check_api_health(self):
        """Check API endpoint health"""
        try:
            # Check critical API endpoints
            endpoints = [
                '/health',
                '/api/leads',
                '/api/sessions',
                '/api/campaigns'
            ]
            
            for endpoint in endpoints:
                start_time = time.time()
                
                # Simulate API check
                response_time = random.uniform(0.1, 0.5)
                
                if response_time > 0.3:
                    self.logger.log(
                        LogLevel.WARNING,
                        LogCategory.PERFORMANCE,
                        f"Slow API response detected for {endpoint}",
                        {'response_time': response_time}
                    )
                
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.log(
                LogLevel.ERROR,
                LogCategory.API_REQUEST,
                "API health check failed",
                {'error': str(e)}
            )
    
    def _check_disk_space(self):
        """Check available disk space"""
        try:
            import shutil
            
            # Get disk usage
            total, used, free = shutil.disk_usage('/')
            free_percentage = (free / total) * 100
            
            if free_percentage < 10:
                self.logger.log(
                    LogLevel.CRITICAL,
                    LogCategory.SYSTEM,
                    "Low disk space detected",
                    {'free_percentage': free_percentage, 'free_gb': free // (1024**3)}
                )
            elif free_percentage < 20:
                self.logger.log(
                    LogLevel.WARNING,
                    LogCategory.SYSTEM,
                    "Disk space warning",
                    {'free_percentage': free_percentage, 'free_gb': free // (1024**3)}
                )
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.log(
                LogLevel.ERROR,
                LogCategory.SYSTEM,
                "Disk space check failed",
                {'error': str(e)}
            )
    
    def _check_memory_usage(self):
        """Check memory usage"""
        try:
            import psutil
            
            # Get memory usage
            memory = psutil.virtual_memory()
            memory_percentage = memory.percent
            
            if memory_percentage > 90:
                self.logger.log(
                    LogLevel.CRITICAL,
                    LogCategory.SYSTEM,
                    "High memory usage detected",
                    {'memory_percentage': memory_percentage, 'available_gb': memory.available // (1024**3)}
                )
            elif memory_percentage > 80:
                self.logger.log(
                    LogLevel.WARNING,
                    LogCategory.SYSTEM,
                    "Memory usage warning",
                    {'memory_percentage': memory_percentage, 'available_gb': memory.available // (1024**3)}
                )
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.log(
                LogLevel.ERROR,
                LogCategory.SYSTEM,
                "Memory usage check failed",
                {'error': str(e)}
            )
    
    def get_system_metrics(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get comprehensive system metrics"""
        try:
            return {
                'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat(),
                'metrics': self.metrics,
                'alerts': self.alerts,
                'health_checks': self.health_checks
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ System metrics retrieval failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
```
