By the end of the day, I had implemented a comprehensive logging and error handling system that provided detailed insights into application behavior, enabled proactive issue detection, and ensured system reliability through intelligent error recovery strategies.

I focused on strengthening error handling and campaign monitoring features. I developed a centralized logging module in the FastAPI backend that captures exceptions during Selenium automation and API operations.

The logs include timestamps, usernames, and message IDs, and are served via API endpoints that the React frontend fetches periodically. I implemented different log levels (INFO, WARNING, ERROR) for better debugging.

Day 14 – Security Implementation and Authentication

Today I focused on implementing comprehensive security measures and authentication systems to protect the application from various threats and ensure secure user access. The goal was to create a robust security infrastructure that could handle authentication, authorization, data protection, and threat prevention.

**Morning Session (4 hours):**
I began by analyzing security requirements and designing a comprehensive security architecture. The system needed to handle:
- User authentication and session management
- Role-based access control (RBAC)
- Data encryption and protection
- API security and rate limiting
- Input validation and sanitization
- Security monitoring and threat detection

I implemented advanced authentication and security systems:

```python
import hashlib
import secrets
import jwt
import bcrypt
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from enum import Enum
import re
import asyncio
from dataclasses import dataclass
import uuid

class UserRole(Enum):
    ADMIN = "admin"
    USER = "user"
    VIEWER = "viewer"
    MODERATOR = "moderator"

class SecurityLevel(Enum):
    LOW = "low"
    MEDIUM = "medium"
    HIGH = "high"
    CRITICAL = "critical"

@dataclass
class User:
    id: str
    username: str
    email: str
    password_hash: str
    role: UserRole
    is_active: bool
    created_at: str
    last_login: Optional[str]
    failed_login_attempts: int
    locked_until: Optional[str]
    security_questions: Dict[str, str]
    two_factor_enabled: bool
    two_factor_secret: Optional[str]

@dataclass
class SecurityEvent:
    id: str
    user_id: Optional[str]
    event_type: str
    severity: SecurityLevel
    description: str
    ip_address: str
    user_agent: str
    timestamp: str
    metadata: Dict[str, Any]

class AdvancedSecurityManager:
    def __init__(self, supabase_client, redis_client):
        self.supabase = supabase_client
        self.redis = redis_client
        self.jwt_secret = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
        self.encryption_key = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
        self.security_events = []
        self.blocked_ips = set()
        self.rate_limits = {}
        
        # Initialize security policies
        self._initialize_security_policies()
    
    def _initialize_security_policies(self):
        """Initialize security policies and configurations"""
        try:
            self.password_policy = {
                'min_length': 8,
                'require_uppercase': True,
                'require_lowercase': True,
                'require_numbers': True,
                'require_special_chars': True,
                'max_age_days': 90
            }
            
            self.session_policy = {
                'timeout_minutes': 30,
                'max_concurrent_sessions': 3,
                'require_reauth_for_sensitive': True
            }
            
            self.rate_limit_policy = {
                'login_attempts_per_hour': 5,
                'api_requests_per_minute': 100,
                'password_reset_attempts_per_day': 3
            }
            
            logger.info("✅ Security policies initialized")
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Security policy initialization failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def hash_password(self, password: str) -> str:
        """Hash password using bcrypt"""
        try:
            salt = bcrypt.gensalt()
            password_hash = bcrypt.hashpw(password.encode('utf-8'), salt)
            return password_hash.decode('utf-8')
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Password hashing failed: {str(e)}")
            raise
    
    def verify_password(self, password: str, password_hash: str) -> bool:
        """Verify password against hash"""
        try:
            return bcrypt.checkpw(password.encode('utf-8'), password_hash.encode('utf-8'))
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Password verification failed: {str(e)}")
            return False
    
    def validate_password_strength(self, password: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Validate password against security policy"""
        try:
            issues = []
            score = 0
            
            # Length check
            if len(password) < self.password_policy['min_length']:
                issues.append(f"Password must be at least {self.password_policy['min_length']} characters")
            else:
                score += 1
            
            # Character type checks
            if self.password_policy['require_uppercase'] and not re.search(r'[A-Z]', password):
                issues.append("Password must contain uppercase letters")
            else:
                score += 1
            
            if self.password_policy['require_lowercase'] and not re.search(r'[a-z]', password):
                issues.append("Password must contain lowercase letters")
            else:
                score += 1
            
            if self.password_policy['require_numbers'] and not re.search(r'\d', password):
                issues.append("Password must contain numbers")
            else:
                score += 1
            
            if self.password_policy['require_special_chars'] and not re.search(r'[!@#$%^&*(),.?":{}|<>]', password):
                issues.append("Password must contain special characters")
            else:
                score += 1
            
            # Common password check
            common_passwords = ['password', '123456', 'admin', 'qwerty', 'letmein']
            if password.lower() in common_passwords:
                issues.append("Password is too common")
                score -= 1
            
            return {
                'is_valid': len(issues) == 0,
                'score': score,
                'issues': issues,
                'strength': 'weak' if score < 3 else 'medium' if score < 5 else 'strong'
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Password validation failed: {str(e)}")
            return {'is_valid': False, 'score': 0, 'issues': ['Validation error'], 'strength': 'weak'}
    
    def generate_jwt_token(self, user: User, expires_in_hours: int = 24) -> str:
        """Generate JWT token for user"""
        try:
            payload = {
                'user_id': user.id,
                'username': user.username,
                'role': user.role.value,
                'iat': datetime.utcnow(),
                'exp': datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(hours=expires_in_hours),
                'jti': str(uuid.uuid4())  # JWT ID for token tracking
            }
            
            token = jwt.encode(payload, self.jwt_secret, algorithm='HS256')
            
            # Store token in Redis for session management
            if self.redis:
                self.redis.setex(
                    f"session:{user.id}:{payload['jti']}",
                    expires_in_hours * 3600,
                    json.dumps({
                        'user_id': user.id,
                        'role': user.role.value,
                        'created_at': datetime.utcnow().isoformat()
                    })
                )
            
            return token
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ JWT token generation failed: {str(e)}")
            raise
    
    def verify_jwt_token(self, token: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Verify and decode JWT token"""
        try:
            payload = jwt.decode(token, self.jwt_secret, algorithms=['HS256'])
            
            # Check if token exists in Redis (for session validation)
            if self.redis:
                session_key = f"session:{payload['user_id']}:{payload['jti']}"
                if not self.redis.exists(session_key):
                    return None
            
            return payload
            
        except jwt.ExpiredSignatureError:
            logger.warning("JWT token expired")
            return None
        except jwt.InvalidTokenError:
            logger.warning("Invalid JWT token")
            return None
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ JWT token verification failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    def authenticate_user(self, username: str, password: str, ip_address: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Authenticate user with comprehensive security checks"""
        try:
            # Check rate limiting
            if not self._check_rate_limit(f"login:{ip_address}"):
                self._log_security_event(
                    None, "rate_limit_exceeded", SecurityLevel.MEDIUM,
                    f"Login rate limit exceeded for IP {ip_address}",
                    ip_address, "Rate limiting"
                )
                return None
            
            # Get user from database
            user = self._get_user_by_username(username)
            if not user:
                self._log_security_event(
                    None, "invalid_login", SecurityLevel.LOW,
                    f"Login attempt with invalid username: {username}",
                    ip_address, "Authentication"
                )
                return None
            
            # Check if user is locked
            if user.locked_until and datetime.now() < datetime.fromisoformat(user.locked_until):
                self._log_security_event(
                    user.id, "account_locked", SecurityLevel.MEDIUM,
                    f"Login attempt on locked account: {username}",
                    ip_address, "Account security"
                )
                return None
            
            # Check if user is active
            if not user.is_active:
                self._log_security_event(
                    user.id, "inactive_account", SecurityLevel.MEDIUM,
                    f"Login attempt on inactive account: {username}",
                    ip_address, "Account security"
                )
                return None
            
            # Verify password
            if not self.verify_password(password, user.password_hash):
                # Increment failed login attempts
                self._increment_failed_logins(user.id)
                
                self._log_security_event(
                    user.id, "invalid_password", SecurityLevel.LOW,
                    f"Invalid password for user: {username}",
                    ip_address, "Authentication"
                )
                return None
            
            # Reset failed login attempts on successful login
            self._reset_failed_logins(user.id)
            
            # Update last login
            self._update_last_login(user.id)
            
            # Generate JWT token
            token = self.generate_jwt_token(user)
            
            # Log successful authentication
            self._log_security_event(
                user.id, "successful_login", SecurityLevel.LOW,
                f"Successful login for user: {username}",
                ip_address, "Authentication"
            )
            
            return {
                'user': {
                    'id': user.id,
                    'username': user.username,
                    'email': user.email,
                    'role': user.role.value,
                    'is_active': user.is_active
                },
                'token': token,
                'expires_in': 24 * 3600  # 24 hours in seconds
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ User authentication failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    def _check_rate_limit(self, key: str) -> bool:
        """Check if rate limit is exceeded"""
        try:
            if not self.redis:
                return True  # No rate limiting if Redis not available
            
            current_time = datetime.now()
            window_start = current_time - timedelta(hours=1)
            
            # Get current count
            count = self.redis.get(f"rate_limit:{key}")
            if count is None:
                count = 0
            else:
                count = int(count)
            
            # Check against limits
            if key.startswith("login:"):
                limit = self.rate_limit_policy['login_attempts_per_hour']
            elif key.startswith("api:"):
                limit = self.rate_limit_policy['api_requests_per_minute']
            else:
                limit = 100  # Default limit
            
            if count >= limit:
                return False
            
            # Increment counter
            self.redis.incr(f"rate_limit:{key}")
            self.redis.expire(f"rate_limit:{key}", 3600)  # Expire in 1 hour
            
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Rate limit check failed: {str(e)}")
            return True  # Allow on error
    
    def _increment_failed_logins(self, user_id: str):
        """Increment failed login attempts for user"""
        try:
            # Get current count
            result = self.supabase.table('users').select('failed_login_attempts').eq('id', user_id).execute()
            if result.data:
                current_attempts = result.data[0]['failed_login_attempts']
                new_attempts = current_attempts + 1
                
                # Update attempts
                self.supabase.table('users').update({
                    'failed_login_attempts': new_attempts
                }).eq('id', user_id).execute()
                
                # Lock account if too many attempts
                if new_attempts >= 5:
                    lock_until = datetime.now() + timedelta(minutes=30)
                    self.supabase.table('users').update({
                        'locked_until': lock_until.isoformat()
                    }).eq('id', user_id).execute()
                    
                    logger.warning(f"Account locked for user {user_id} due to failed login attempts")
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Failed login increment failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _reset_failed_logins(self, user_id: str):
        """Reset failed login attempts for user"""
        try:
            self.supabase.table('users').update({
                'failed_login_attempts': 0,
                'locked_until': None
            }).eq('id', user_id).execute()
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Failed login reset failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _update_last_login(self, user_id: str):
        """Update last login timestamp for user"""
        try:
            self.supabase.table('users').update({
                'last_login': datetime.now().isoformat()
            }).eq('id', user_id).execute()
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Last login update failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _get_user_by_username(self, username: str) -> Optional[User]:
        """Get user by username"""
        try:
            result = self.supabase.table('users').select('*').eq('username', username).execute()
            
            if result.data:
                user_data = result.data[0]
                return User(
                    id=user_data['id'],
                    username=user_data['username'],
                    email=user_data['email'],
                    password_hash=user_data['password_hash'],
                    role=UserRole(user_data['role']),
                    is_active=user_data['is_active'],
                    created_at=user_data['created_at'],
                    last_login=user_data.get('last_login'),
                    failed_login_attempts=user_data.get('failed_login_attempts', 0),
                    locked_until=user_data.get('locked_until'),
                    security_questions=user_data.get('security_questions', {}),
                    two_factor_enabled=user_data.get('two_factor_enabled', False),
                    two_factor_secret=user_data.get('two_factor_secret')
                )
            
            return None
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ User retrieval failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    def _log_security_event(self, user_id: Optional[str], event_type: str, 
                           severity: SecurityLevel, description: str, 
                           ip_address: str, category: str):
        """Log security event"""
        try:
            event = SecurityEvent(
                id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
                user_id=user_id,
                event_type=event_type,
                severity=severity,
                description=description,
                ip_address=ip_address,
                user_agent="Unknown",  # Would be extracted from request
                timestamp=datetime.now().isoformat(),
                metadata={'category': category}
            )
            
            # Store in memory
            self.security_events.append(event)
            
            # Store in database
            if self.supabase:
                self.supabase.table('security_events').insert({
                    'id': event.id,
                    'user_id': event.user_id,
                    'event_type': event.event_type,
                    'severity': event.severity.value,
                    'description': event.description,
                    'ip_address': event.ip_address,
                    'user_agent': event.user_agent,
                    'timestamp': event.timestamp,
                    'metadata': event.metadata
                }).execute()
            
            # Alert on critical events
            if severity == SecurityLevel.CRITICAL:
                self._send_security_alert(event)
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Security event logging failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _send_security_alert(self, event: SecurityEvent):
        """Send security alert for critical events"""
        try:
            # In a real implementation, this would send notifications
            logger.critical(f"🚨 SECURITY ALERT: {event.description}")
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Security alert failed: {str(e)}")
```

**Afternoon Session (4 hours):**
I implemented comprehensive authorization and data protection:

```python
class AuthorizationManager:
    def __init__(self, security_manager: AdvancedSecurityManager):
        self.security_manager = security_manager
        self.permissions = {
            UserRole.ADMIN: ['*'],  # All permissions
            UserRole.MODERATOR: [
                'leads:read', 'leads:update', 'leads:delete',
                'sessions:read', 'sessions:update',
                'campaigns:read', 'campaigns:create', 'campaigns:update'
            ],
            UserRole.USER: [
                'leads:read', 'sessions:read',
                'campaigns:read', 'campaigns:create'
            ],
            UserRole.VIEWER: [
                'leads:read', 'sessions:read', 'campaigns:read'
            ]
        }
    
    def check_permission(self, user_role: UserRole, resource: str, action: str) -> bool:
        """Check if user has permission for resource action"""
        try:
            user_permissions = self.permissions.get(user_role, [])
            
            # Check for wildcard permission
            if '*' in user_permissions:
                return True
            
            # Check for specific permission
            permission = f"{resource}:{action}"
            return permission in user_permissions
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Permission check failed: {str(e)}")
            return False
    
    def authorize_request(self, token: str, resource: str, action: str) -> bool:
        """Authorize API request"""
        try:
            # Verify token
            payload = self.security_manager.verify_jwt_token(token)
            if not payload:
                return False
            
            # Check permission
            user_role = UserRole(payload['role'])
            return self.check_permission(user_role, resource, action)
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Request authorization failed: {str(e)}")
            return False

class DataProtectionManager:
    def __init__(self, encryption_key: str):
        self.encryption_key = encryption_key
        self.sensitive_fields = [
            'password', 'email', 'phone', 'ssn', 'credit_card',
            'api_key', 'secret', 'token', 'private_key'
        ]
    
    def encrypt_sensitive_data(self, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Encrypt sensitive data fields"""
        try:
            from cryptography.fernet import Fernet
            import base64
            
            # Create encryption key
            key = base64.urlsafe_b64encode(self.encryption_key.encode()[:32])
            fernet = Fernet(key)
            
            encrypted_data = {}
            for key, value in data.items():
                if self._is_sensitive_field(key):
                    if isinstance(value, str):
                        encrypted_data[key] = fernet.encrypt(value.encode()).decode()
                    else:
                        encrypted_data[key] = value
                else:
                    encrypted_data[key] = value
            
            return encrypted_data
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Data encryption failed: {str(e)}")
            return data
    
    def decrypt_sensitive_data(self, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Decrypt sensitive data fields"""
        try:
            from cryptography.fernet import Fernet
            import base64
            
            # Create decryption key
            key = base64.urlsafe_b64encode(self.encryption_key.encode()[:32])
            fernet = Fernet(key)
            
            decrypted_data = {}
            for key, value in data.items():
                if self._is_sensitive_field(key) and isinstance(value, str):
                    try:
                        decrypted_data[key] = fernet.decrypt(value.encode()).decode()
                    except:
                        decrypted_data[key] = value  # Return original if decryption fails
                else:
                    decrypted_data[key] = value
            
            return decrypted_data
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Data decryption failed: {str(e)}")
            return data
    
    def _is_sensitive_field(self, field_name: str) -> bool:
        """Check if field contains sensitive data"""
        field_lower = field_name.lower()
        return any(sensitive in field_lower for sensitive in self.sensitive_fields)
    
    def sanitize_input(self, data: str) -> str:
        """Sanitize user input to prevent XSS and injection attacks"""
        try:
            import html
            import re
            
            # HTML escape
            sanitized = html.escape(data)
            
            # Remove potentially dangerous patterns
            dangerous_patterns = [
                r'<script.*?</script>',
                r'javascript:',
                r'vbscript:',
                r'onload=',
                r'onerror=',
                r'onclick=',
                r'onmouseover='
            ]
            
            for pattern in dangerous_patterns:
                sanitized = re.sub(pattern, '', sanitized, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
            
            return sanitized.strip()
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Input sanitization failed: {str(e)}")
            return data

class SecurityMonitor:
    def __init__(self, security_manager: AdvancedSecurityManager):
        self.security_manager = security_manager
        self.threat_indicators = {}
        self.alert_thresholds = {
            'failed_logins_per_hour': 10,
            'api_requests_per_minute': 1000,
            'suspicious_patterns_per_hour': 5
        }
    
    def monitor_security_events(self):
        """Monitor security events for threats"""
        try:
            # Analyze recent security events
            recent_events = self.security_manager.security_events[-100:]
            
            # Check for brute force attacks
            self._check_brute_force_attacks(recent_events)
            
            # Check for suspicious patterns
            self._check_suspicious_patterns(recent_events)
            
            # Check for privilege escalation attempts
            self._check_privilege_escalation(recent_events)
            
            # Update threat indicators
            self._update_threat_indicators(recent_events)
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Security monitoring failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _check_brute_force_attacks(self, events: List[SecurityEvent]):
        """Check for brute force attack patterns"""
        try:
            # Group events by IP address
            ip_events = {}
            for event in events:
                if event.event_type == 'invalid_password':
                    ip = event.ip_address
                    if ip not in ip_events:
                        ip_events[ip] = []
                    ip_events[ip].append(event)
            
            # Check for high failure rates
            for ip, ip_event_list in ip_events.items():
                if len(ip_event_list) >= 5:  # 5 failed attempts
                    self.security_manager._log_security_event(
                        None, "brute_force_attack", SecurityLevel.CRITICAL,
                        f"Brute force attack detected from IP {ip}",
                        ip, "Threat detection"
                    )
                    
                    # Block IP address
                    self.security_manager.blocked_ips.add(ip)
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Brute force detection failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _check_suspicious_patterns(self, events: List[SecurityEvent]):
        """Check for suspicious activity patterns"""
        try:
            # Check for rapid API requests
            api_events = [e for e in events if e.event_type == 'api_request']
            if len(api_events) > 100:  # More than 100 API requests
                self.security_manager._log_security_event(
                    None, "suspicious_api_activity", SecurityLevel.HIGH,
                    f"High volume API activity detected: {len(api_events)} requests",
                    "Unknown", "Threat detection"
                )
            
            # Check for unusual login times
            login_events = [e for e in events if e.event_type == 'successful_login']
            for event in login_events:
                login_time = datetime.fromisoformat(event.timestamp)
                if login_time.hour < 6 or login_time.hour > 22:  # Outside normal hours
                    self.security_manager._log_security_event(
                        event.user_id, "unusual_login_time", SecurityLevel.MEDIUM,
                        f"Login at unusual time: {login_time}",
                        event.ip_address, "Behavioral analysis"
                    )
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Suspicious pattern detection failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _check_privilege_escalation(self, events: List[SecurityEvent]):
        """Check for privilege escalation attempts"""
        try:
            # Look for attempts to access admin functions
            admin_events = [e for e in events if 'admin' in e.description.lower()]
            if len(admin_events) > 3:
                self.security_manager._log_security_event(
                    None, "privilege_escalation_attempt", SecurityLevel.HIGH,
                    f"Multiple admin access attempts detected: {len(admin_events)}",
                    "Unknown", "Threat detection"
                )
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Privilege escalation detection failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _update_threat_indicators(self, events: List[SecurityEvent]):
        """Update threat indicators based on events"""
        try:
            current_time = datetime.now()
            
            # Count events by type
            event_counts = {}
            for event in events:
                event_type = event.event_type
                if event_type not in event_counts:
                    event_counts[event_type] = 0
                event_counts[event_type] += 1
            
            # Update threat level
            total_events = len(events)
            if total_events > 50:
                self.threat_indicators['threat_level'] = 'HIGH'
            elif total_events > 20:
                self.threat_indicators['threat_level'] = 'MEDIUM'
            else:
                self.threat_indicators['threat_level'] = 'LOW'
            
            # Update indicators
            self.threat_indicators.update({
                'total_events': total_events,
                'event_counts': event_counts,
                'last_updated': current_time.isoformat()
            })
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Threat indicator update failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def get_security_report(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Generate comprehensive security report"""
        try:
            return {
                'threat_indicators': self.threat_indicators,
                'blocked_ips': list(self.security_manager.blocked_ips),
                'recent_events': len(self.security_manager.security_events),
                'recommendations': self._generate_security_recommendations()
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Security report generation failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    def _generate_security_recommendations(self) -> List[str]:
        """Generate security recommendations"""
        try:
            recommendations = []
            
            # Check threat level
            if self.threat_indicators.get('threat_level') == 'HIGH':
                recommendations.append("Consider implementing additional security measures")
            
            # Check blocked IPs
            if len(self.security_manager.blocked_ips) > 10:
                recommendations.append("Review blocked IP addresses and consider IP whitelisting")
            
            # Check event volume
            if self.threat_indicators.get('total_events', 0) > 100:
                recommendations.append("High security event volume - consider log analysis")
            
            return recommendations
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Security recommendation generation failed: {str(e)}")
            return []
```

I implemented advanced API integration systems:

```python
import requests
import asyncio
import aiohttp
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import json
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum
import hashlib
import hmac

class APIService(Enum):
    INSTAGRAM = "instagram"
    FACEBOOK = "facebook"
    TWITTER = "twitter"
    EMAIL = "email"
    SMS = "sms"
    PAYMENT = "payment"
    ANALYTICS = "analytics"

class APIStatus(Enum):
    ACTIVE = "active"
    INACTIVE = "inactive"
    RATE_LIMITED = "rate_limited"
    ERROR = "error"
    MAINTENANCE = "maintenance"

@dataclass
class APIResponse:
    success: bool
    data: Any
    status_code: int
    headers: Dict[str, str]
    response_time: float
    rate_limit_remaining: Optional[int]
    rate_limit_reset: Optional[int]
    error_message: Optional[str]

@dataclass
class APIConfig:
    base_url: str
    api_key: str
    secret_key: Optional[str]
    rate_limit: int
    timeout: int
    retry_attempts: int
    retry_delay: float

class APIIntegrationManager:
    def __init__(self):
        self.api_configs = {}
        self.api_statuses = {}
        self.rate_limiters = {}
        self.circuit_breakers = {}
        self.retry_strategies = {}
        
        # Initialize API configurations
        self._initialize_api_configs()
        
        # Initialize rate limiters
        self._initialize_rate_limiters()
        
        # Initialize circuit breakers
        self._initialize_circuit_breakers()
    
    def _initialize_api_configs(self):
        """Initialize API configurations"""
        try:
            self.api_configs = {
                APIService.INSTAGRAM: APIConfig(
                    base_url="https://graph.instagram.com",
                    api_key="your_instagram_token",
                    secret_key=None,
                    rate_limit=200,
                    timeout=30,
                    retry_attempts=3,
                    retry_delay=1.0
                ),
                APIService.FACEBOOK: APIConfig(
                    base_url="https://graph.facebook.com",
                    api_key="your_facebook_token",
                    secret_key=None,
                    rate_limit=200,
                    timeout=30,
                    retry_attempts=3,
                    retry_delay=1.0
                ),
                APIService.EMAIL: APIConfig(
                    base_url="https://api.sendgrid.com",
                    api_key="your_sendgrid_key",
                    secret_key=None,
                    rate_limit=100,
                    timeout=15,
                    retry_attempts=2,
                    retry_delay=0.5
                ),
                APIService.SMS: APIConfig(
                    base_url="https://api.twilio.com",
                    api_key="your_twilio_sid",
                    secret_key="your_twilio_token",
                    rate_limit=100,
                    timeout=15,
                    retry_attempts=2,
                    retry_delay=0.5
                ),
                APIService.PAYMENT: APIConfig(
                    base_url="https://api.stripe.com",
                    api_key="your_stripe_key",
                    secret_key="your_stripe_secret",
                    rate_limit=100,
                    timeout=30,
                    retry_attempts=3,
                    retry_delay=1.0
                )
            }
            
            logger.info("✅ API configurations initialized")
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ API configuration initialization failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _initialize_rate_limiters(self):
        """Initialize rate limiters for each API"""
        try:
            for service, config in self.api_configs.items():
                self.rate_limiters[service] = {
                    'requests_made': 0,
                    'window_start': time.time(),
                    'limit': config.rate_limit
                }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Rate limiter initialization failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _initialize_circuit_breakers(self):
        """Initialize circuit breakers for each API"""
        try:
            for service in self.api_configs.keys():
                self.circuit_breakers[service] = {
                    'state': 'closed',  # closed, open, half-open
                    'failure_count': 0,
                    'last_failure_time': None,
                    'failure_threshold': 5,
                    'timeout': 60  # seconds
                }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Circuit breaker initialization failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def make_api_request(self, service: APIService, endpoint: str, 
                             method: str = 'GET', data: Dict = None, 
                             headers: Dict = None) -> APIResponse:
        """Make API request with comprehensive error handling"""
        try:
            # Check circuit breaker
            if not self._check_circuit_breaker(service):
                return APIResponse(
                    success=False,
                    data=None,
                    status_code=503,
                    headers={},
                    response_time=0,
                    rate_limit_remaining=None,
                    rate_limit_reset=None,
                    error_message="Circuit breaker open"
                )
            
            # Check rate limiting
            if not self._check_rate_limit(service):
                return APIResponse(
                    success=False,
                    data=None,
                    status_code=429,
                    headers={},
                    response_time=0,
                    rate_limit_remaining=0,
                    rate_limit_reset=None,
                    error_message="Rate limit exceeded"
                )
            
            # Get API configuration
            config = self.api_configs.get(service)
            if not config:
                raise ValueError(f"API configuration not found for service: {service}")
            
            # Prepare request
            url = f"{config.base_url}{endpoint}"
            request_headers = self._prepare_headers(service, headers)
            request_data = self._prepare_data(service, data)
            
            # Make request with retry logic
            response = await self._make_request_with_retry(
                service, url, method, request_data, request_headers, config
            )
            
            # Update rate limiter
            self._update_rate_limiter(service)
            
            # Update circuit breaker
            self._update_circuit_breaker(service, response.success)
            
            return response
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ API request failed for {service}: {str(e)}")
            return APIResponse(
                success=False,
                data=None,
                status_code=500,
                headers={},
                response_time=0,
                rate_limit_remaining=None,
                rate_limit_reset=None,
                error_message=str(e)
            )
    
    def _check_circuit_breaker(self, service: APIService) -> bool:
        """Check if circuit breaker allows request"""
        try:
            breaker = self.circuit_breakers.get(service)
            if not breaker:
                return True
            
            current_time = time.time()
            
            if breaker['state'] == 'closed':
                return True
            elif breaker['state'] == 'open':
                # Check if timeout has passed
                if (breaker['last_failure_time'] and 
                    current_time - breaker['last_failure_time'] > breaker['timeout']):
                    breaker['state'] = 'half-open'
                    return True
                return False
            elif breaker['state'] == 'half-open':
                return True
            
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Circuit breaker check failed: {str(e)}")
            return True
    
    def _check_rate_limit(self, service: APIService) -> bool:
        """Check if rate limit allows request"""
        try:
            limiter = self.rate_limiters.get(service)
            if not limiter:
                return True
            
            current_time = time.time()
            window_duration = 3600  # 1 hour window
            
            # Reset window if needed
            if current_time - limiter['window_start'] > window_duration:
                limiter['requests_made'] = 0
                limiter['window_start'] = current_time
            
            # Check if limit exceeded
            if limiter['requests_made'] >= limiter['limit']:
                return False
            
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Rate limit check failed: {str(e)}")
            return True
    
    def _prepare_headers(self, service: APIService, custom_headers: Dict = None) -> Dict[str, str]:
        """Prepare headers for API request"""
        try:
            config = self.api_configs.get(service)
            if not config:
                return custom_headers or {}
            
            headers = {
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                'User-Agent': 'InstagramScraper/1.0'
            }
            
            # Add API key
            if service == APIService.INSTAGRAM:
                headers['Authorization'] = f"Bearer {config.api_key}"
            elif service == APIService.FACEBOOK:
                headers['Authorization'] = f"Bearer {config.api_key}"
            elif service == APIService.EMAIL:
                headers['Authorization'] = f"Bearer {config.api_key}"
            elif service == APIService.SMS:
                headers['Authorization'] = f"Basic {self._encode_basic_auth(config.api_key, config.secret_key)}"
            elif service == APIService.PAYMENT:
                headers['Authorization'] = f"Bearer {config.api_key}"
            
            # Add custom headers
            if custom_headers:
                headers.update(custom_headers)
            
            return headers
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Header preparation failed: {str(e)}")
            return custom_headers or {}
    
    def _prepare_data(self, service: APIService, data: Dict = None) -> Dict:
        """Prepare data for API request"""
        try:
            if not data:
                return {}
            
            # Service-specific data preparation
            if service == APIService.INSTAGRAM:
                return self._prepare_instagram_data(data)
            elif service == APIService.EMAIL:
                return self._prepare_email_data(data)
            elif service == APIService.SMS:
                return self._prepare_sms_data(data)
            elif service == APIService.PAYMENT:
                return self._prepare_payment_data(data)
            
            return data
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Data preparation failed: {str(e)}")
            return data or {}
    
    def _prepare_instagram_data(self, data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Prepare data for Instagram API"""
        try:
            # Instagram-specific data formatting
            formatted_data = {}
            
            if 'fields' in data:
                formatted_data['fields'] = ','.join(data['fields'])
            
            if 'limit' in data:
                formatted_data['limit'] = data['limit']
            
            if 'after' in data:
                formatted_data['after'] = data['after']
            
            return formatted_data
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Instagram data preparation failed: {str(e)}")
            return data
    
    def _prepare_email_data(self, data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Prepare data for email API"""
        try:
            # Email-specific data formatting
            formatted_data = {
                'personalizations': [{
                    'to': [{'email': email} for email in data.get('to', [])],
                    'subject': data.get('subject', '')
                }],
                'from': {'email': data.get('from', 'noreply@example.com')},
                'content': [{
                    'type': 'text/plain',
                    'value': data.get('body', '')
                }]
            }
            
            return formatted_data
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Email data preparation failed: {str(e)}")
            return data
    
    def _prepare_sms_data(self, data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Prepare data for SMS API"""
        try:
            # SMS-specific data formatting
            formatted_data = {
                'To': data.get('to', ''),
                'From': data.get('from', ''),
                'Body': data.get('body', '')
            }
            
            return formatted_data
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ SMS data preparation failed: {str(e)}")
            return data
    
    def _prepare_payment_data(self, data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """Prepare data for payment API"""
        try:
            # Payment-specific data formatting
            formatted_data = {
                'amount': data.get('amount', 0),
                'currency': data.get('currency', 'usd'),
                'description': data.get('description', ''),
                'metadata': data.get('metadata', {})
            }
            
            return formatted_data
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Payment data preparation failed: {str(e)}")
            return data
    
    async def _make_request_with_retry(self, service: APIService, url: str, 
                                    method: str, data: Dict, headers: Dict, 
                                    config: APIConfig) -> APIResponse:
        """Make request with retry logic"""
        try:
            last_exception = None
            
            for attempt in range(config.retry_attempts):
                try:
                    start_time = time.time()
                    
                    async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
                        if method.upper() == 'GET':
                            async with session.get(url, headers=headers, params=data, timeout=config.timeout) as response:
                                response_data = await response.json()
                        elif method.upper() == 'POST':
                            async with session.post(url, headers=headers, json=data, timeout=config.timeout) as response:
                                response_data = await response.json()
                        elif method.upper() == 'PUT':
                            async with session.put(url, headers=headers, json=data, timeout=config.timeout) as response:
                                response_data = await response.json()
                        elif method.upper() == 'DELETE':
                            async with session.delete(url, headers=headers, timeout=config.timeout) as response:
                                response_data = await response.json()
                        else:
                            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported HTTP method: {method}")
                    
                    response_time = time.time() - start_time
                    
                    # Extract rate limit information
                    rate_limit_remaining = response.headers.get('X-RateLimit-Remaining')
                    rate_limit_reset = response.headers.get('X-RateLimit-Reset')
                    
                    return APIResponse(
                        success=response.status < 400,
                        data=response_data,
                        status_code=response.status,
                        headers=dict(response.headers),
                        response_time=response_time,
                        rate_limit_remaining=int(rate_limit_remaining) if rate_limit_remaining else None,
                        rate_limit_reset=int(rate_limit_reset) if rate_limit_reset else None,
                        error_message=None if response.status < 400 else str(response_data)
                    )
                    
                except Exception as e:
                    last_exception = e
                    if attempt < config.retry_attempts - 1:
                        await asyncio.sleep(config.retry_delay * (2 ** attempt))  # Exponential backoff
                        continue
                    else:
                        raise e
            
            # If all retries failed
            return APIResponse(
                success=False,
                data=None,
                status_code=500,
                headers={},
                response_time=0,
                rate_limit_remaining=None,
                rate_limit_reset=None,
                error_message=str(last_exception)
            )
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Request with retry failed: {str(e)}")
            return APIResponse(
                success=False,
                data=None,
                status_code=500,
                headers={},
                response_time=0,
                rate_limit_remaining=None,
                rate_limit_reset=None,
                error_message=str(e)
            )
    
    def _update_rate_limiter(self, service: APIService):
        """Update rate limiter after successful request"""
        try:
            limiter = self.rate_limiters.get(service)
            if limiter:
                limiter['requests_made'] += 1
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Rate limiter update failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _update_circuit_breaker(self, service: APIService, success: bool):
        """Update circuit breaker based on request result"""
        try:
            breaker = self.circuit_breakers.get(service)
            if not breaker:
                return
            
            if success:
                # Reset failure count on success
                breaker['failure_count'] = 0
                if breaker['state'] == 'half-open':
                    breaker['state'] = 'closed'
            else:
                # Increment failure count on failure
                breaker['failure_count'] += 1
                breaker['last_failure_time'] = time.time()
                
                # Open circuit if threshold exceeded
                if breaker['failure_count'] >= breaker['failure_threshold']:
                    breaker['state'] = 'open'
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Circuit breaker update failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _encode_basic_auth(self, username: str, password: str) -> str:
        """Encode basic authentication"""
        try:
            import base64
            credentials = f"{username}:{password}"
            encoded = base64.b64encode(credentials.encode()).decode()
            return encoded
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Basic auth encoding failed: {str(e)}")
            return ""
    
    def get_api_status(self, service: APIService) -> APIStatus:
        """Get current status of API service"""
        try:
            breaker = self.circuit_breakers.get(service)
            if not breaker:
                return APIStatus.ACTIVE
            
            if breaker['state'] == 'open':
                return APIStatus.ERROR
            elif breaker['state'] == 'half-open':
                return APIStatus.ACTIVE
            else:
                return APIStatus.ACTIVE
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ API status check failed: {str(e)}")
            return APIStatus.ERROR
    
    def get_rate_limit_info(self, service: APIService) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get rate limit information for API service"""
        try:
            limiter = self.rate_limiters.get(service)
            if not limiter:
                return {}
            
            return {
                'requests_made': limiter['requests_made'],
                'limit': limiter['limit'],
                'remaining': limiter['limit'] - limiter['requests_made'],
                'window_start': limiter['window_start']
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Rate limit info retrieval failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
```

**Afternoon Session (4 hours):**
I implemented specialized service integrations:

```python
class InstagramAPIService:
    def __init__(self, integration_manager: APIIntegrationManager):
        self.integration_manager = integration_manager
        self.service = APIService.INSTAGRAM
    
    async def get_user_profile(self, username: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get Instagram user profile"""
        try:
            endpoint = f"/{username}"
            data = {
                'fields': ['id', 'username', 'account_type', 'media_count', 'followers_count', 'follows_count']
            }
            
            response = await self.integration_manager.make_api_request(
                self.service, endpoint, 'GET', data
            )
            
            if response.success:
                return response.data
            else:
                logger.error(f"❌ Instagram profile fetch failed: {response.error_message}")
                return {}
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Instagram profile fetch failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    async def get_user_media(self, user_id: str, limit: int = 25) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Get Instagram user media"""
        try:
            endpoint = f"/{user_id}/media"
            data = {
                'fields': ['id', 'caption', 'media_type', 'media_url', 'thumbnail_url', 'timestamp', 'like_count', 'comments_count'],
                'limit': limit
            }
            
            response = await self.integration_manager.make_api_request(
                self.service, endpoint, 'GET', data
            )
            
            if response.success:
                return response.data.get('data', [])
            else:
                logger.error(f"❌ Instagram media fetch failed: {response.error_message}")
                return []
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Instagram media fetch failed: {str(e)}")
            return []
    
    async def get_media_insights(self, media_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get Instagram media insights"""
        try:
            endpoint = f"/{media_id}/insights"
            data = {
                'metric': ['impressions', 'reach', 'likes', 'comments', 'saves', 'shares']
            }
            
            response = await self.integration_manager.make_api_request(
                self.service, endpoint, 'GET', data
            )
            
            if response.success:
                return response.data
            else:
                logger.error(f"❌ Instagram insights fetch failed: {response.error_message}")
                return {}
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Instagram insights fetch failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}

class EmailService:
    def __init__(self, integration_manager: APIIntegrationManager):
        self.integration_manager = integration_manager
        self.service = APIService.EMAIL
    
    async def send_email(self, to: List[str], subject: str, body: str, 
                        from_email: str = None) -> bool:
        """Send email notification"""
        try:
            endpoint = "/v3/mail/send"
            data = {
                'to': to,
                'subject': subject,
                'body': body,
                'from': from_email or 'noreply@phyixinsta.com'
            }
            
            response = await self.integration_manager.make_api_request(
                self.service, endpoint, 'POST', data
            )
            
            if response.success:
                logger.info(f"✅ Email sent successfully to {len(to)} recipients")
                return True
            else:
                logger.error(f"❌ Email sending failed: {response.error_message}")
                return False
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Email sending failed: {str(e)}")
            return False
    
    async def send_campaign_notification(self, campaign_name: str, status: str, 
                                       recipients: List[str]) -> bool:
        """Send campaign status notification"""
        try:
            subject = f"Campaign Update: {campaign_name}"
            body = f"""
            Your campaign '{campaign_name}' has been updated.
            
            Status: {status}
            Time: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}
            
            Please check your dashboard for more details.
            """
            
            return await self.send_email(recipients, subject, body)
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Campaign notification failed: {str(e)}")
            return False

class SMSService:
    def __init__(self, integration_manager: APIIntegrationManager):
        self.integration_manager = integration_manager
        self.service = APIService.SMS
    
    async def send_sms(self, to: str, message: str, from_number: str = None) -> bool:
        """Send SMS notification"""
        try:
            endpoint = "/2010-04-01/Accounts/{AccountSid}/Messages.json"
            data = {
                'to': to,
                'body': message,
                'from': from_number or '+1234567890'
            }
            
            response = await self.integration_manager.make_api_request(
                self.service, endpoint, 'POST', data
            )
            
            if response.success:
                logger.info(f"✅ SMS sent successfully to {to}")
                return True
            else:
                logger.error(f"❌ SMS sending failed: {response.error_message}")
                return False
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ SMS sending failed: {str(e)}")
            return False
    
    async def send_alert_sms(self, message: str, admin_phone: str) -> bool:
        """Send alert SMS to admin"""
        try:
            alert_message = f"🚨 ALERT: {message}"
            return await self.send_sms(admin_phone, alert_message)
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Alert SMS failed: {str(e)}")
            return False

class PaymentService:
    def __init__(self, integration_manager: APIIntegrationManager):
        self.integration_manager = integration_manager
        self.service = APIService.PAYMENT
    
    async def create_payment_intent(self, amount: int, currency: str = 'usd', 
                                  description: str = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Create payment intent"""
        try:
            endpoint = "/v1/payment_intents"
            data = {
                'amount': amount,
                'currency': currency,
                'description': description or 'Instagram Scraper Subscription'
            }
            
            response = await self.integration_manager.make_api_request(
                self.service, endpoint, 'POST', data
            )
            
            if response.success:
                return response.data
            else:
                logger.error(f"❌ Payment intent creation failed: {response.error_message}")
                return {}
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Payment intent creation failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    async def create_subscription(self, customer_id: str, price_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Create subscription"""
        try:
            endpoint = "/v1/subscriptions"
            data = {
                'customer': customer_id,
                'items': [{'price': price_id}],
                'payment_behavior': 'default_incomplete',
                'payment_settings': {'save_default_payment_method': 'on_subscription'},
                'expand': ['latest_invoice.payment_intent']
            }
            
            response = await self.integration_manager.make_api_request(
                self.service, endpoint, 'POST', data
            )
            
            if response.success:
                return response.data
            else:
                logger.error(f"❌ Subscription creation failed: {response.error_message}")
                return {}
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Subscription creation failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}

class AnalyticsService:
    def __init__(self, integration_manager: APIIntegrationManager):
        self.integration_manager = integration_manager
        self.service = APIService.ANALYTICS
    
    async def track_event(self, event_name: str, properties: Dict[str, Any], 
                         user_id: str = None) -> bool:
        """Track analytics event"""
        try:
            endpoint = "/v1/events"
            data = {
                'event': event_name,
                'properties': properties,
                'user_id': user_id,
                'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat()
            }
            
            response = await self.integration_manager.make_api_request(
                self.service, endpoint, 'POST', data
            )
            
            if response.success:
                logger.info(f"✅ Analytics event tracked: {event_name}")
                return True
            else:
                logger.error(f"❌ Analytics tracking failed: {response.error_message}")
                return False
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Analytics tracking failed: {str(e)}")
            return False
    
    async def track_campaign_event(self, campaign_id: str, event_type: str, 
                                 properties: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        """Track campaign-specific event"""
        try:
            event_name = f"campaign_{event_type}"
            event_properties = {
                'campaign_id': campaign_id,
                'event_type': event_type,
                **properties
            }
            
            return await self.track_event(event_name, event_properties)
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Campaign analytics tracking failed: {str(e)}")
            return False

class NotificationManager:
    def __init__(self, email_service: EmailService, sms_service: SMSService):
        self.email_service = email_service
        self.sms_service = sms_service
        self.notification_preferences = {}
    
    async def send_notification(self, notification_type: str, message: str, 
                              recipients: List[str], channels: List[str] = None) -> bool:
        """Send notification through multiple channels"""
        try:
            if not channels:
                channels = ['email']
            
            success_count = 0
            
            for channel in channels:
                if channel == 'email':
                    if await self.email_service.send_email(recipients, "Notification", message):
                        success_count += 1
                elif channel == 'sms':
                    for recipient in recipients:
                        if await self.sms_service.send_sms(recipient, message):
                            success_count += 1
            
            logger.info(f"✅ Notifications sent: {success_count}/{len(recipients) * len(channels)}")
            return success_count > 0
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Notification sending failed: {str(e)}")
            return False
    
    async def send_system_alert(self, alert_type: str, message: str, 
                              admin_contacts: List[str]) -> bool:
        """Send system alert to administrators"""
        try:
            alert_message = f"🚨 SYSTEM ALERT: {alert_type}\n\n{message}"
            
            # Send via email
            email_success = await self.email_service.send_email(
                admin_contacts, f"System Alert: {alert_type}", alert_message
            )
            
            # Send via SMS to primary admin
            sms_success = False
            if admin_contacts:
                sms_success = await self.sms_service.send_alert_sms(
                    alert_message, admin_contacts[0]
                )
            
            return email_success or sms_success
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ System alert failed: {str(e)}")
            return False
```
