Day 16 – Multi-Account Management

Today I focused on implementing a comprehensive multi-account management system that could handle multiple Instagram accounts simultaneously while maintaining security, session isolation, and efficient resource utilization. The goal was to create a robust account management infrastructure that could scale to support numerous users and accounts.

**Morning Session (4 hours):**
I began by analyzing the multi-account requirements and designing a comprehensive account management architecture. The system needed to handle:
- Multiple Instagram account authentication and session management
- Account isolation and security
- Resource allocation and load balancing
- Account switching and context management
- Session persistence and recovery
- Account monitoring and health checks

I implemented an advanced multi-account management system:

```python
import asyncio
import threading
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
from enum import Enum
import json
import uuid
import hashlib
import base64
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor

class AccountStatus(Enum):
    ACTIVE = "active"
    INACTIVE = "inactive"
    SUSPENDED = "suspended"
    RATE_LIMITED = "rate_limited"
    ERROR = "error"
    MAINTENANCE = "maintenance"

class AccountType(Enum):
    PERSONAL = "personal"
    BUSINESS = "business"
    CREATOR = "creator"
    TEST = "test"

@dataclass
class InstagramAccount:
    id: str
    username: str
    password: str
    email: str
    account_type: AccountType
    status: AccountStatus
    is_verified: bool
    followers_count: int
    following_count: int
    posts_count: int
    bio: str
    profile_picture_url: str
    cookies: Dict[str, Any]
    session_data: Dict[str, Any]
    last_activity: str
    created_at: str
    updated_at: str
    metadata: Dict[str, Any]

@dataclass
class AccountSession:
    account_id: str
    session_id: str
    driver_instance: Any
    is_active: bool
    created_at: str
    last_used: str
    activity_count: int
    resource_usage: Dict[str, Any]

class MultiAccountManager:
    def __init__(self, supabase_client, redis_client):
        self.supabase = supabase_client
        self.redis = redis_client
        self.accounts = {}
        self.active_sessions = {}
        self.account_pools = {}
        self.resource_limits = {
            'max_concurrent_sessions': 10,
            'max_requests_per_hour': 1000,
            'max_memory_usage_mb': 512
        }
        self.session_timeout = 1800  # 30 minutes
        
        # Initialize account management
        self._initialize_account_management()
        
        # Start background tasks
        self._start_background_tasks()
    
    def _initialize_account_management(self):
        """Initialize account management system"""
        try:
            # Load existing accounts from database
            self._load_accounts_from_database()
            
            # Initialize account pools
            self._initialize_account_pools()
            
            # Setup resource monitoring
            self._setup_resource_monitoring()
            
            logger.info("✅ Multi-account management initialized")
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Account management initialization failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _load_accounts_from_database(self):
        """Load accounts from database"""
        try:
            if not self.supabase:
                return
            
            result = self.supabase.table('instagram_accounts').select('*').execute()
            
            for account_data in result.data:
                account = InstagramAccount(
                    id=account_data['id'],
                    username=account_data['username'],
                    password=account_data['password'],
                    email=account_data['email'],
                    account_type=AccountType(account_data['account_type']),
                    status=AccountStatus(account_data['status']),
                    is_verified=account_data.get('is_verified', False),
                    followers_count=account_data.get('followers_count', 0),
                    following_count=account_data.get('following_count', 0),
                    posts_count=account_data.get('posts_count', 0),
                    bio=account_data.get('bio', ''),
                    profile_picture_url=account_data.get('profile_picture_url', ''),
                    cookies=json.loads(account_data.get('cookies', '{}')),
                    session_data=json.loads(account_data.get('session_data', '{}')),
                    last_activity=account_data.get('last_activity', ''),
                    created_at=account_data['created_at'],
                    updated_at=account_data['updated_at'],
                    metadata=json.loads(account_data.get('metadata', '{}'))
                )
                
                self.accounts[account.id] = account
            
            logger.info(f"✅ Loaded {len(self.accounts)} accounts from database")
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Account loading failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _initialize_account_pools(self):
        """Initialize account pools for load balancing"""
        try:
            # Group accounts by type
            for account_type in AccountType:
                self.account_pools[account_type] = []
            
            # Assign accounts to pools
            for account in self.accounts.values():
                if account.status == AccountStatus.ACTIVE:
                    self.account_pools[account.account_type].append(account.id)
            
            logger.info("✅ Account pools initialized")
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Account pool initialization failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _setup_resource_monitoring(self):
        """Setup resource monitoring for accounts"""
        try:
            self.resource_monitor = {
                'memory_usage': {},
                'cpu_usage': {},
                'network_usage': {},
                'session_counts': {}
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Resource monitoring setup failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _start_background_tasks(self):
        """Start background monitoring tasks"""
        try:
            # Start session cleanup task
            cleanup_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._cleanup_expired_sessions)
            cleanup_thread.daemon = True
            cleanup_thread.start()
            
            # Start resource monitoring task
            monitor_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._monitor_resources)
            monitor_thread.daemon = True
            monitor_thread.start()
            
            # Start account health check task
            health_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._check_account_health)
            health_thread.daemon = True
            health_thread.start()
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Background task startup failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def create_account(self, username: str, password: str, email: str, 
                           account_type: AccountType = AccountType.PERSONAL) -> Optional[str]:
        """Create new Instagram account"""
        try:
            # Check if account already exists
            existing_account = self._find_account_by_username(username)
            if existing_account:
                logger.warning(f"Account {username} already exists")
                return existing_account.id
            
            # Create account ID
            account_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
            
            # Create account object
            account = InstagramAccount(
                id=account_id,
                username=username,
                password=password,
                email=email,
                account_type=account_type,
                status=AccountStatus.INACTIVE,
                is_verified=False,
                followers_count=0,
                following_count=0,
                posts_count=0,
                bio='',
                profile_picture_url='',
                cookies={},
                session_data={},
                last_activity='',
                created_at=datetime.now().isoformat(),
                updated_at=datetime.now().isoformat(),
                metadata={}
            )
            
            # Save to database
            if self.supabase:
                await self._save_account_to_database(account)
            
            # Add to local storage
            self.accounts[account_id] = account
            
            # Add to account pool
            self.account_pools[account_type].append(account_id)
            
            logger.info(f"✅ Created account: {username}")
            return account_id
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Account creation failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    async def authenticate_account(self, account_id: str) -> bool:
        """Authenticate Instagram account"""
        try:
            account = self.accounts.get(account_id)
            if not account:
                logger.error(f"Account {account_id} not found")
                return False
            
            # Check if account is already authenticated
            if account.status == AccountStatus.ACTIVE:
                logger.info(f"Account {account.username} already authenticated")
                return True
            
            # Create new session
            session = await self._create_account_session(account_id)
            if not session:
                return False
            
            # Test authentication
            auth_success = await self._test_account_authentication(session)
            if not auth_success:
                await self._close_account_session(account_id)
                return False
            
            # Update account status
            account.status = AccountStatus.ACTIVE
            account.last_activity = datetime.now().isoformat()
            account.updated_at = datetime.now().isoformat()
            
            # Save to database
            if self.supabase:
                await self._update_account_in_database(account)
            
            logger.info(f"✅ Account {account.username} authenticated successfully")
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Account authentication failed: {str(e)}")
            return False
    
    async def _create_account_session(self, account_id: str) -> Optional[AccountSession]:
        """Create new account session"""
        try:
            account = self.accounts.get(account_id)
            if not account:
                return None
            
            # Check resource limits
            if not self._check_resource_limits():
                logger.warning("Resource limits exceeded, cannot create session")
                return None
            
            # Create session ID
            session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
            
            # Create WebDriver instance
            driver = await self._create_webdriver_instance(account)
            if not driver:
                return None
            
            # Create session object
            session = AccountSession(
                account_id=account_id,
                session_id=session_id,
                driver_instance=driver,
                is_active=True,
                created_at=datetime.now().isoformat(),
                last_used=datetime.now().isoformat(),
                activity_count=0,
                resource_usage={
                    'memory_mb': 0,
                    'cpu_percent': 0,
                    'network_bytes': 0
                }
            )
            
            # Store session
            self.active_sessions[account_id] = session
            
            # Update resource monitoring
            self._update_resource_monitoring(account_id, 'session_created')
            
            logger.info(f"✅ Created session for account {account.username}")
            return session
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Session creation failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    async def _create_webdriver_instance(self, account: InstagramAccount) -> Optional[Any]:
        """Create WebDriver instance for account"""
        try:
            from selenium import webdriver
            from selenium.webdriver.chrome.options import Options
            from selenium.webdriver.common.by import By
            from selenium.webdriver.support.ui import WebDriverWait
            from selenium.webdriver.support import expected_conditions as EC
            
            # Configure Chrome options
            chrome_options = Options()
            chrome_options.add_argument('--headless')
            chrome_options.add_argument('--no-sandbox')
            chrome_options.add_argument('--disable-dev-shm-usage')
            chrome_options.add_argument('--disable-gpu')
            chrome_options.add_argument('--window-size=1920,1080')
            chrome_options.add_argument('--user-agent=Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36')
            
            # Create driver instance
            driver = webdriver.Chrome(options=chrome_options)
            
            # Set timeouts
            driver.implicitly_wait(10)
            driver.set_page_load_timeout(30)
            
            # Load cookies if available
            if account.cookies:
                await self._load_account_cookies(driver, account)
            
            return driver
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ WebDriver creation failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    async def _load_account_cookies(self, driver, account: InstagramAccount):
        """Load cookies for account"""
        try:
            # Navigate to Instagram first
            driver.get("https://www.instagram.com")
            
            # Load cookies
            for cookie in account.cookies:
                try:
                    driver.add_cookie(cookie)
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.warning(f"Failed to load cookie: {str(e)}")
                    continue
            
            # Refresh page to apply cookies
            driver.refresh()
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Cookie loading failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def _test_account_authentication(self, session: AccountSession) -> bool:
        """Test if account is properly authenticated"""
        try:
            driver = session.driver_instance
            
            # Navigate to Instagram
            driver.get("https://www.instagram.com")
            
            # Check if logged in by looking for profile elements
            try:
                # Wait for profile link or user menu
                WebDriverWait(driver, 10).until(
                    EC.presence_of_element_located((By.CSS_SELECTOR, "a[href*='/accounts/edit/']"))
                )
                return True
            except:
                # Try alternative method
                try:
                    WebDriverWait(driver, 5).until(
                        EC.presence_of_element_located((By.CSS_SELECTOR, "svg[aria-label='Home']"))
                    )
                    return True
                except:
                    return False
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Authentication test failed: {str(e)}")
            return False
    
    async def get_account_session(self, account_id: str) -> Optional[AccountSession]:
        """Get active session for account"""
        try:
            session = self.active_sessions.get(account_id)
            if not session:
                logger.warning(f"No active session for account {account_id}")
                return None
            
            # Check if session is still valid
            if not self._is_session_valid(session):
                await self._close_account_session(account_id)
                return None
            
            # Update session activity
            session.last_used = datetime.now().isoformat()
            session.activity_count += 1
            
            return session
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Session retrieval failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    def _is_session_valid(self, session: AccountSession) -> bool:
        """Check if session is still valid"""
        try:
            # Check if session is active
            if not session.is_active:
                return False
            
            # Check session timeout
            last_used = datetime.fromisoformat(session.last_used)
            if datetime.now() - last_used > timedelta(seconds=self.session_timeout):
                return False
            
            # Check if driver is still responsive
            try:
                session.driver_instance.current_url
                return True
            except:
                return False
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Session validation failed: {str(e)}")
            return False
    
    async def _close_account_session(self, account_id: str):
        """Close account session"""
        try:
            session = self.active_sessions.get(account_id)
            if not session:
                return
            
            # Close WebDriver
            try:
                session.driver_instance.quit()
            except:
                pass
            
            # Remove from active sessions
            del self.active_sessions[account_id]
            
            # Update resource monitoring
            self._update_resource_monitoring(account_id, 'session_closed')
            
            logger.info(f"✅ Closed session for account {account_id}")
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Session closure failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _check_resource_limits(self) -> bool:
        """Check if resource limits allow new session"""
        try:
            # Check concurrent session limit
            if len(self.active_sessions) >= self.resource_limits['max_concurrent_sessions']:
                return False
            
            # Check memory usage
            total_memory = sum(
                session.resource_usage.get('memory_mb', 0) 
                for session in self.active_sessions.values()
            )
            if total_memory >= self.resource_limits['max_memory_usage_mb']:
                return False
            
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Resource limit check failed: {str(e)}")
            return False
    
    def _update_resource_monitoring(self, account_id: str, event: str):
        """Update resource monitoring"""
        try:
            if event == 'session_created':
                self.resource_monitor['session_counts'][account_id] = 1
            elif event == 'session_closed':
                if account_id in self.resource_monitor['session_counts']:
                    del self.resource_monitor['session_counts'][account_id]
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Resource monitoring update failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _cleanup_expired_sessions(self):
        """Cleanup expired sessions"""
        try:
            while True:
                current_time = datetime.now()
                expired_sessions = []
                
                for account_id, session in self.active_sessions.items():
                    last_used = datetime.fromisoformat(session.last_used)
                    if current_time - last_used > timedelta(seconds=self.session_timeout):
                        expired_sessions.append(account_id)
                
                # Close expired sessions
                for account_id in expired_sessions:
                    asyncio.create_task(self._close_account_session(account_id))
                
                # Sleep for 5 minutes
                threading.Event().wait(300)
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Session cleanup failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _monitor_resources(self):
        """Monitor resource usage"""
        try:
            while True:
                # Monitor memory usage
                for account_id, session in self.active_sessions.items():
                    try:
                        # Get memory usage (simplified)
                        memory_usage = 50  # MB per session
                        session.resource_usage['memory_mb'] = memory_usage
                    except:
                        pass
                
                # Sleep for 1 minute
                threading.Event().wait(60)
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Resource monitoring failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _check_account_health(self):
        """Check account health"""
        try:
            while True:
                for account_id, session in self.active_sessions.items():
                    try:
                        # Test account responsiveness
                        driver = session.driver_instance
                        driver.get("https://www.instagram.com")
                        
                        # Update last activity
                        account = self.accounts.get(account_id)
                        if account:
                            account.last_activity = datetime.now().isoformat()
                        
                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.warning(f"Account {account_id} health check failed: {str(e)}")
                        # Mark account as problematic
                        account = self.accounts.get(account_id)
                        if account:
                            account.status = AccountStatus.ERROR
                
                # Sleep for 10 minutes
                threading.Event().wait(600)
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Account health check failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def get_available_account(self, account_type: AccountType = None) -> Optional[str]:
        """Get available account for use"""
        try:
            # Filter by account type if specified
            if account_type:
                available_accounts = self.account_pools.get(account_type, [])
            else:
                available_accounts = []
                for pool in self.account_pools.values():
                    available_accounts.extend(pool)
            
            # Find account without active session
            for account_id in available_accounts:
                if account_id not in self.active_sessions:
                    account = self.accounts.get(account_id)
                    if account and account.status == AccountStatus.ACTIVE:
                        return account_id
            
            return None
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Available account retrieval failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    async def get_account_statistics(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get account statistics"""
        try:
            total_accounts = len(self.accounts)
            active_accounts = len([a for a in self.accounts.values() if a.status == AccountStatus.ACTIVE])
            active_sessions = len(self.active_sessions)
            
            return {
                'total_accounts': total_accounts,
                'active_accounts': active_accounts,
                'active_sessions': active_sessions,
                'account_types': {
                    account_type.value: len(accounts) 
                    for account_type, accounts in self.account_pools.items()
                },
                'resource_usage': self.resource_monitor
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Account statistics retrieval failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
```

**Afternoon Session (4 hours):**
I implemented account switching and context management:

```python
class AccountContextManager:
    def __init__(self, account_manager: MultiAccountManager):
        self.account_manager = account_manager
        self.current_context = None
        self.context_history = []
        self.context_switches = 0
    
    async def switch_to_account(self, account_id: str) -> bool:
        """Switch to specific account context"""
        try:
            # Get account session
            session = await self.account_manager.get_account_session(account_id)
            if not session:
                # Try to authenticate account
                auth_success = await self.account_manager.authenticate_account(account_id)
                if not auth_success:
                    return False
                session = await self.account_manager.get_account_session(account_id)
            
            if not session:
                return False
            
            # Update context
            self.current_context = {
                'account_id': account_id,
                'session_id': session.session_id,
                'switched_at': datetime.now().isoformat()
            }
            
            # Add to history
            self.context_history.append(self.current_context.copy())
            self.context_switches += 1
            
            # Keep only last 10 switches
            if len(self.context_history) > 10:
                self.context_history = self.context_history[-10:]
            
            logger.info(f"✅ Switched to account {account_id}")
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Account switching failed: {str(e)}")
            return False
    
    async def execute_with_account(self, account_id: str, operation_func, *args, **kwargs):
        """Execute operation with specific account"""
        try:
            # Switch to account
            switch_success = await self.switch_to_account(account_id)
            if not switch_success:
                return None
            
            # Execute operation
            result = await operation_func(*args, **kwargs)
            
            return result
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Account operation execution failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    def get_current_context(self) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Get current account context"""
        return self.current_context
    
    def get_context_history(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Get context switch history"""
        return self.context_history

class AccountLoadBalancer:
    def __init__(self, account_manager: MultiAccountManager):
        self.account_manager = account_manager
        self.load_balancing_strategy = 'round_robin'
        self.account_weights = {}
        self.last_used_account = None
    
    async def get_best_account(self, account_type: AccountType = None) -> Optional[str]:
        """Get best account based on load balancing strategy"""
        try:
            if self.load_balancing_strategy == 'round_robin':
                return await self._get_round_robin_account(account_type)
            elif self.load_balancing_strategy == 'least_used':
                return await self._get_least_used_account(account_type)
            elif self.load_balancing_strategy == 'weighted':
                return await self._get_weighted_account(account_type)
            else:
                return await self._get_round_robin_account(account_type)
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Account selection failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    async def _get_round_robin_account(self, account_type: AccountType = None) -> Optional[str]:
        """Get account using round-robin strategy"""
        try:
            # Get available accounts
            if account_type:
                available_accounts = self.account_manager.account_pools.get(account_type, [])
            else:
                available_accounts = []
                for pool in self.account_manager.account_pools.values():
                    available_accounts.extend(pool)
            
            if not available_accounts:
                return None
            
            # Find next account in round-robin
            if self.last_used_account:
                try:
                    current_index = available_accounts.index(self.last_used_account)
                    next_index = (current_index + 1) % len(available_accounts)
                except ValueError:
                    next_index = 0
            else:
                next_index = 0
            
            selected_account = available_accounts[next_index]
            self.last_used_account = selected_account
            
            return selected_account
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Round-robin account selection failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    async def _get_least_used_account(self, account_type: AccountType = None) -> Optional[str]:
        """Get least used account"""
        try:
            # Get available accounts
            if account_type:
                available_accounts = self.account_manager.account_pools.get(account_type, [])
            else:
                available_accounts = []
                for pool in self.account_manager.account_pools.values():
                    available_accounts.extend(pool)
            
            if not available_accounts:
                return None
            
            # Find account with least activity
            least_used_account = None
            min_activity = float('inf')
            
            for account_id in available_accounts:
                account = self.account_manager.accounts.get(account_id)
                if account:
                    # Calculate activity score
                    activity_score = self._calculate_activity_score(account)
                    if activity_score < min_activity:
                        min_activity = activity_score
                        least_used_account = account_id
            
            return least_used_account
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Least used account selection failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    def _calculate_activity_score(self, account: InstagramAccount) -> float:
        """Calculate activity score for account"""
        try:
            # Base score
            score = 0
            
            # Add session activity
            session = self.account_manager.active_sessions.get(account.id)
            if session:
                score += session.activity_count
            
            # Add time since last activity
            if account.last_activity:
                last_activity = datetime.fromisoformat(account.last_activity)
                hours_since_activity = (datetime.now() - last_activity).total_seconds() / 3600
                score -= hours_since_activity  # Negative score for recent activity
            
            return score
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Activity score calculation failed: {str(e)}")
            return 0
    
    async def _get_weighted_account(self, account_type: AccountType = None) -> Optional[str]:
        """Get account using weighted strategy"""
        try:
            # Get available accounts
            if account_type:
                available_accounts = self.account_manager.account_pools.get(account_type, [])
            else:
                available_accounts = []
                for pool in self.account_manager.account_pools.values():
                    available_accounts.extend(pool)
            
            if not available_accounts:
                return None
            
            # Calculate weights
            weights = []
            for account_id in available_accounts:
                account = self.account_manager.accounts.get(account_id)
                if account:
                    weight = self.account_weights.get(account_id, 1.0)
                    weights.append(weight)
                else:
                    weights.append(0.0)
            
            # Select account based on weights
            import random
            selected_account = random.choices(available_accounts, weights=weights)[0]
            
            return selected_account
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Weighted account selection failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    def set_account_weight(self, account_id: str, weight: float):
        """Set weight for account"""
        try:
            self.account_weights[account_id] = weight
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Account weight setting failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def set_load_balancing_strategy(self, strategy: str):
        """Set load balancing strategy"""
        try:
            if strategy in ['round_robin', 'least_used', 'weighted']:
                self.load_balancing_strategy = strategy
                logger.info(f"✅ Load balancing strategy set to: {strategy}")
            else:
                logger.warning(f"Invalid load balancing strategy: {strategy}")
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Load balancing strategy setting failed: {str(e)}")

class AccountSecurityManager:
    def __init__(self, account_manager: MultiAccountManager):
        self.account_manager = account_manager
        self.security_policies = {
            'max_failed_attempts': 3,
            'lockout_duration_minutes': 30,
            'session_timeout_minutes': 30,
            'require_2fa': False
        }
        self.failed_attempts = {}
        self.locked_accounts = {}
    
    async def validate_account_access(self, account_id: str, operation: str) -> bool:
        """Validate account access for operation"""
        try:
            # Check if account is locked
            if account_id in self.locked_accounts:
                lockout_time = self.locked_accounts[account_id]
                if datetime.now() < lockout_time:
                    logger.warning(f"Account {account_id} is locked until {lockout_time}")
                    return False
                else:
                    # Remove from locked accounts
                    del self.locked_accounts[account_id]
            
            # Check account status
            account = self.account_manager.accounts.get(account_id)
            if not account or account.status != AccountStatus.ACTIVE:
                return False
            
            # Check session validity
            session = self.account_manager.active_sessions.get(account_id)
            if not session or not session.is_active:
                return False
            
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Account access validation failed: {str(e)}")
            return False
    
    async def record_failed_attempt(self, account_id: str, operation: str):
        """Record failed attempt for account"""
        try:
            if account_id not in self.failed_attempts:
                self.failed_attempts[account_id] = []
            
            self.failed_attempts[account_id].append({
                'operation': operation,
                'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat()
            })
            
            # Check if account should be locked
            recent_failures = [
                attempt for attempt in self.failed_attempts[account_id]
                if datetime.now() - datetime.fromisoformat(attempt['timestamp']) < timedelta(hours=1)
            ]
            
            if len(recent_failures) >= self.security_policies['max_failed_attempts']:
                await self._lock_account(account_id)
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Failed attempt recording failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def _lock_account(self, account_id: str):
        """Lock account due to security violations"""
        try:
            lockout_duration = timedelta(minutes=self.security_policies['lockout_duration_minutes'])
            lockout_time = datetime.now() + lockout_duration
            
            self.locked_accounts[account_id] = lockout_time
            
            # Update account status
            account = self.account_manager.accounts.get(account_id)
            if account:
                account.status = AccountStatus.SUSPENDED
            
            logger.warning(f"🔒 Account {account_id} locked until {lockout_time}")
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Account locking failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def unlock_account(self, account_id: str):
        """Unlock account"""
        try:
            if account_id in self.locked_accounts:
                del self.locked_accounts[account_id]
            
            # Reset failed attempts
            if account_id in self.failed_attempts:
                del self.failed_attempts[account_id]
            
            # Update account status
            account = self.account_manager.accounts.get(account_id)
            if account:
                account.status = AccountStatus.ACTIVE
            
            logger.info(f"🔓 Account {account_id} unlocked")
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Account unlocking failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def get_security_status(self, account_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get security status for account"""
        try:
            status = {
                'is_locked': account_id in self.locked_accounts,
                'failed_attempts': len(self.failed_attempts.get(account_id, [])),
                'lockout_time': self.locked_accounts.get(account_id),
                'recent_failures': []
            }
            
            if account_id in self.failed_attempts:
                recent_failures = [
                    attempt for attempt in self.failed_attempts[account_id]
                    if datetime.now() - datetime.fromisoformat(attempt['timestamp']) < timedelta(hours=1)
                ]
                status['recent_failures'] = recent_failures
            
            return status
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Security status retrieval failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
```

I implemented an advanced analytics and reporting system:

```python
import asyncio
import json
import time
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional, Tuple
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
from enum import Enum
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
from collections import defaultdict, Counter
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import seaborn as sns
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text
import plotly.graph_objects as go
import plotly.express as px
from plotly.subplots import make_subplots

class MetricType(Enum):
    CAMPAIGN_PERFORMANCE = "campaign_performance"
    USER_ENGAGEMENT = "user_engagement"
    SYSTEM_PERFORMANCE = "system_performance"
    ERROR_ANALYSIS = "error_analysis"
    GEOGRAPHIC_INSIGHTS = "geographic_insights"
    REVENUE_TRACKING = "revenue_tracking"

class ReportType(Enum):
    DAILY_SUMMARY = "daily_summary"
    WEEKLY_ANALYSIS = "weekly_analysis"
    MONTHLY_REPORT = "monthly_report"
    CUSTOM_RANGE = "custom_range"
    REAL_TIME_DASHBOARD = "real_time_dashboard"

@dataclass
class AnalyticsEvent:
    event_id: str
    event_type: str
    timestamp: str
    user_id: str
    session_id: str
    campaign_id: str
    account_id: str
    data: Dict[str, Any]
    metadata: Dict[str, Any]

@dataclass
class PerformanceMetric:
    metric_name: str
    value: float
    unit: str
    timestamp: str
    category: str
    tags: List[str]

@dataclass
class CampaignAnalytics:
    campaign_id: str
    campaign_name: str
    start_date: str
    end_date: str
    total_messages_sent: int
    successful_deliveries: int
    failed_deliveries: int
    response_rate: float
    engagement_rate: float
    conversion_rate: float
    cost_per_message: float
    total_cost: float
    roi: float
    demographics: Dict[str, Any]
    geographic_distribution: Dict[str, Any]
    performance_trends: List[Dict[str, Any]]

class AdvancedAnalyticsEngine:
    def __init__(self, supabase_client, redis_client):
        self.supabase = supabase_client
        self.redis = redis_client
        self.events_buffer = []
        self.metrics_cache = {}
        self.report_cache = {}
        self.analytics_config = {
            'buffer_size': 1000,
            'flush_interval': 60,  # seconds
            'retention_days': 365,
            'real_time_enabled': True
        }
        
        # Initialize analytics engine
        self._initialize_analytics_engine()
        
        # Start background processing
        self._start_background_processing()
    
    def _initialize_analytics_engine(self):
        """Initialize analytics engine"""
        try:
            # Setup event tracking
            self._setup_event_tracking()
            
            # Initialize metrics collection
            self._initialize_metrics_collection()
            
            # Setup report generation
            self._setup_report_generation()
            
            # Initialize visualization tools
            self._initialize_visualization_tools()
            
            logger.info("✅ Advanced analytics engine initialized")
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Analytics engine initialization failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _setup_event_tracking(self):
        """Setup event tracking system"""
        try:
            self.event_trackers = {
                'campaign_events': [],
                'user_events': [],
                'system_events': [],
                'error_events': []
            }
            
            # Setup event handlers
            self.event_handlers = {
                'campaign_started': self._handle_campaign_started,
                'campaign_completed': self._handle_campaign_completed,
                'message_sent': self._handle_message_sent,
                'message_delivered': self._handle_message_delivered,
                'user_engaged': self._handle_user_engaged,
                'error_occurred': self._handle_error_occurred,
                'system_metric': self._handle_system_metric
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Event tracking setup failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _initialize_metrics_collection(self):
        """Initialize metrics collection system"""
        try:
            self.metrics_collectors = {
                'campaign_metrics': CampaignMetricsCollector(),
                'user_metrics': UserMetricsCollector(),
                'system_metrics': SystemMetricsCollector(),
                'error_metrics': ErrorMetricsCollector()
            }
            
            # Setup metric aggregation
            self.metric_aggregators = {
                'hourly': HourlyAggregator(),
                'daily': DailyAggregator(),
                'weekly': WeeklyAggregator(),
                'monthly': MonthlyAggregator()
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Metrics collection initialization failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _setup_report_generation(self):
        """Setup report generation system"""
        try:
            self.report_generators = {
                'campaign_reports': CampaignReportGenerator(),
                'user_reports': UserReportGenerator(),
                'system_reports': SystemReportGenerator(),
                'executive_reports': ExecutiveReportGenerator()
            }
            
            # Setup report templates
            self.report_templates = {
                'daily_summary': DailySummaryTemplate(),
                'weekly_analysis': WeeklyAnalysisTemplate(),
                'monthly_report': MonthlyReportTemplate(),
                'executive_dashboard': ExecutiveDashboardTemplate()
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Report generation setup failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _initialize_visualization_tools(self):
        """Initialize visualization tools"""
        try:
            # Setup plotting libraries
            plt.style.use('seaborn-v0_8')
            sns.set_palette("husl")
            
            # Setup plotly configuration
            self.plotly_config = {
                'displayModeBar': True,
                'displaylogo': False,
                'modeBarButtonsToRemove': ['pan2d', 'lasso2d', 'select2d']
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Visualization tools initialization failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _start_background_processing(self):
        """Start background processing tasks"""
        try:
            # Start event processing
            event_thread = asyncio.create_task(self._process_events())
            
            # Start metrics aggregation
            metrics_thread = asyncio.create_task(self._aggregate_metrics())
            
            # Start report generation
            report_thread = asyncio.create_task(self._generate_reports())
            
            # Start cache cleanup
            cleanup_thread = asyncio.create_task(self._cleanup_cache())
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Background processing startup failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def track_event(self, event_type: str, data: Dict[str, Any], 
                         user_id: str = None, session_id: str = None, 
                         campaign_id: str = None, account_id: str = None):
        """Track analytics event"""
        try:
            # Create event
            event = AnalyticsEvent(
                event_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
                event_type=event_type,
                timestamp=datetime.now().isoformat(),
                user_id=user_id or '',
                session_id=session_id or '',
                campaign_id=campaign_id or '',
                account_id=account_id or '',
                data=data,
                metadata={
                    'source': 'analytics_engine',
                    'version': '1.0.0',
                    'environment': 'production'
                }
            )
            
            # Add to buffer
            self.events_buffer.append(event)
            
            # Process if buffer is full
            if len(self.events_buffer) >= self.analytics_config['buffer_size']:
                await self._flush_events()
            
            # Handle event
            if event_type in self.event_handlers:
                await self.event_handlers[event_type](event)
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Event tracking failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def _flush_events(self):
        """Flush events to storage"""
        try:
            if not self.events_buffer:
                return
            
            # Save to database
            if self.supabase:
                events_data = [asdict(event) for event in self.events_buffer]
                await self._save_events_to_database(events_data)
            
            # Save to cache
            if self.redis:
                await self._save_events_to_cache(events_data)
            
            # Clear buffer
            self.events_buffer.clear()
            
            logger.info(f"✅ Flushed {len(events_data)} events to storage")
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Event flushing failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def _save_events_to_database(self, events_data: List[Dict[str, Any]]):
        """Save events to database"""
        try:
            result = self.supabase.table('analytics_events').insert(events_data).execute()
            logger.info(f"✅ Saved {len(events_data)} events to database")
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Database event saving failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def _save_events_to_cache(self, events_data: List[Dict[str, Any]]):
        """Save events to cache"""
        try:
            for event in events_data:
                cache_key = f"analytics:event:{event['event_id']}"
                await self.redis.setex(cache_key, 3600, json.dumps(event))
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Cache event saving failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def _process_events(self):
        """Process events in background"""
        try:
            while True:
                # Process buffered events
                if self.events_buffer:
                    await self._flush_events()
                
                # Sleep for flush interval
                await asyncio.sleep(self.analytics_config['flush_interval'])
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Event processing failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def _aggregate_metrics(self):
        """Aggregate metrics in background"""
        try:
            while True:
                # Aggregate hourly metrics
                await self._aggregate_hourly_metrics()
                
                # Aggregate daily metrics
                await self._aggregate_daily_metrics()
                
                # Sleep for 1 hour
                await asyncio.sleep(3600)
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Metrics aggregation failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def _generate_reports(self):
        """Generate reports in background"""
        try:
            while True:
                # Generate daily reports
                await self._generate_daily_reports()
                
                # Generate weekly reports
                await self._generate_weekly_reports()
                
                # Sleep for 24 hours
                await asyncio.sleep(86400)
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Report generation failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def _cleanup_cache(self):
        """Cleanup cache in background"""
        try:
            while True:
                # Cleanup old cache entries
                await self._cleanup_old_cache_entries()
                
                # Sleep for 1 hour
                await asyncio.sleep(3600)
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Cache cleanup failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def _handle_campaign_started(self, event: AnalyticsEvent):
        """Handle campaign started event"""
        try:
            # Track campaign metrics
            await self.track_metric('campaigns_started', 1, 'count')
            await self.track_metric('active_campaigns', 1, 'count', increment=True)
            
            # Update campaign analytics
            campaign_data = event.data
            await self._update_campaign_analytics(event.campaign_id, 'started', campaign_data)
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Campaign started handling failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def _handle_campaign_completed(self, event: AnalyticsEvent):
        """Handle campaign completed event"""
        try:
            # Track campaign metrics
            await self.track_metric('campaigns_completed', 1, 'count')
            await self.track_metric('active_campaigns', -1, 'count', increment=True)
            
            # Calculate campaign performance
            performance_data = await self._calculate_campaign_performance(event.campaign_id)
            await self._update_campaign_analytics(event.campaign_id, 'completed', performance_data)
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Campaign completed handling failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def _handle_message_sent(self, event: AnalyticsEvent):
        """Handle message sent event"""
        try:
            # Track message metrics
            await self.track_metric('messages_sent', 1, 'count')
            
            # Track by campaign
            if event.campaign_id:
                await self.track_metric(f'campaign_{event.campaign_id}_messages_sent', 1, 'count')
            
            # Track by account
            if event.account_id:
                await self.track_metric(f'account_{event.account_id}_messages_sent', 1, 'count')
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Message sent handling failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def _handle_message_delivered(self, event: AnalyticsEvent):
        """Handle message delivered event"""
        try:
            # Track delivery metrics
            await self.track_metric('messages_delivered', 1, 'count')
            
            # Calculate delivery rate
            delivery_rate = await self._calculate_delivery_rate()
            await self.track_metric('delivery_rate', delivery_rate, 'percentage')
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Message delivered handling failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def _handle_user_engaged(self, event: AnalyticsEvent):
        """Handle user engagement event"""
        try:
            # Track engagement metrics
            await self.track_metric('user_engagements', 1, 'count')
            
            # Track engagement rate
            engagement_rate = await self._calculate_engagement_rate()
            await self.track_metric('engagement_rate', engagement_rate, 'percentage')
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ User engagement handling failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def _handle_error_occurred(self, event: AnalyticsEvent):
        """Handle error event"""
        try:
            # Track error metrics
            await self.track_metric('errors_occurred', 1, 'count')
            
            # Track error rate
            error_rate = await self._calculate_error_rate()
            await self.track_metric('error_rate', error_rate, 'percentage')
            
            # Categorize error
            error_type = event.data.get('error_type', 'unknown')
            await self.track_metric(f'errors_{error_type}', 1, 'count')
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Error handling failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def _handle_system_metric(self, event: AnalyticsEvent):
        """Handle system metric event"""
        try:
            # Track system metrics
            metric_name = event.data.get('metric_name')
            metric_value = event.data.get('metric_value')
            metric_unit = event.data.get('metric_unit', 'count')
            
            if metric_name and metric_value is not None:
                await self.track_metric(metric_name, metric_value, metric_unit)
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ System metric handling failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def track_metric(self, metric_name: str, value: float, unit: str, 
                          increment: bool = False, tags: List[str] = None):
        """Track performance metric"""
        try:
            # Create metric
            metric = PerformanceMetric(
                metric_name=metric_name,
                value=value,
                unit=unit,
                timestamp=datetime.now().isoformat(),
                category='performance',
                tags=tags or []
            )
            
            # Update cache
            cache_key = f"metrics:{metric_name}"
            if increment:
                current_value = self.metrics_cache.get(cache_key, 0)
                self.metrics_cache[cache_key] = current_value + value
            else:
                self.metrics_cache[cache_key] = value
            
            # Save to database
            if self.supabase:
                await self._save_metric_to_database(metric)
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Metric tracking failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def _save_metric_to_database(self, metric: PerformanceMetric):
        """Save metric to database"""
        try:
            metric_data = asdict(metric)
            result = self.supabase.table('performance_metrics').insert(metric_data).execute()
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Metric database saving failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def _calculate_campaign_performance(self, campaign_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Calculate campaign performance metrics"""
        try:
            # Get campaign data
            campaign_data = await self._get_campaign_data(campaign_id)
            if not campaign_data:
                return {}
            
            # Calculate metrics
            total_messages = campaign_data.get('total_messages', 0)
            successful_deliveries = campaign_data.get('successful_deliveries', 0)
            failed_deliveries = campaign_data.get('failed_deliveries', 0)
            responses = campaign_data.get('responses', 0)
            
            # Calculate rates
            delivery_rate = (successful_deliveries / total_messages * 100) if total_messages > 0 else 0
            response_rate = (responses / successful_deliveries * 100) if successful_deliveries > 0 else 0
            engagement_rate = (responses / total_messages * 100) if total_messages > 0 else 0
            
            # Calculate costs
            cost_per_message = campaign_data.get('cost_per_message', 0)
            total_cost = total_messages * cost_per_message
            
            # Calculate ROI
            revenue = campaign_data.get('revenue', 0)
            roi = ((revenue - total_cost) / total_cost * 100) if total_cost > 0 else 0
            
            return {
                'total_messages': total_messages,
                'successful_deliveries': successful_deliveries,
                'failed_deliveries': failed_deliveries,
                'delivery_rate': delivery_rate,
                'response_rate': response_rate,
                'engagement_rate': engagement_rate,
                'total_cost': total_cost,
                'revenue': revenue,
                'roi': roi
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Campaign performance calculation failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    async def _calculate_delivery_rate(self) -> float:
        """Calculate overall delivery rate"""
        try:
            # Get delivery metrics
            messages_sent = self.metrics_cache.get('messages_sent', 0)
            messages_delivered = self.metrics_cache.get('messages_delivered', 0)
            
            if messages_sent > 0:
                return (messages_delivered / messages_sent) * 100
            return 0
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Delivery rate calculation failed: {str(e)}")
            return 0
    
    async def _calculate_engagement_rate(self) -> float:
        """Calculate overall engagement rate"""
        try:
            # Get engagement metrics
            messages_delivered = self.metrics_cache.get('messages_delivered', 0)
            user_engagements = self.metrics_cache.get('user_engagements', 0)
            
            if messages_delivered > 0:
                return (user_engagements / messages_delivered) * 100
            return 0
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Engagement rate calculation failed: {str(e)}")
            return 0
    
    async def _calculate_error_rate(self) -> float:
        """Calculate overall error rate"""
        try:
            # Get error metrics
            total_operations = self.metrics_cache.get('total_operations', 0)
            errors_occurred = self.metrics_cache.get('errors_occurred', 0)
            
            if total_operations > 0:
                return (errors_occurred / total_operations) * 100
            return 0
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Error rate calculation failed: {str(e)}")
            return 0
    
    async def _update_campaign_analytics(self, campaign_id: str, status: str, data: Dict[str, Any]):
        """Update campaign analytics"""
        try:
            # Get existing analytics
            analytics = await self._get_campaign_analytics(campaign_id)
            if not analytics:
                analytics = CampaignAnalytics(
                    campaign_id=campaign_id,
                    campaign_name='',
                    start_date='',
                    end_date='',
                    total_messages_sent=0,
                    successful_deliveries=0,
                    failed_deliveries=0,
                    response_rate=0.0,
                    engagement_rate=0.0,
                    conversion_rate=0.0,
                    cost_per_message=0.0,
                    total_cost=0.0,
                    roi=0.0,
                    demographics={},
                    geographic_distribution={},
                    performance_trends=[]
                )
            
            # Update based on status
            if status == 'started':
                analytics.start_date = datetime.now().isoformat()
                analytics.campaign_name = data.get('campaign_name', '')
            elif status == 'completed':
                analytics.end_date = datetime.now().isoformat()
                analytics.total_messages_sent = data.get('total_messages', 0)
                analytics.successful_deliveries = data.get('successful_deliveries', 0)
                analytics.failed_deliveries = data.get('failed_deliveries', 0)
                analytics.response_rate = data.get('response_rate', 0.0)
                analytics.engagement_rate = data.get('engagement_rate', 0.0)
                analytics.total_cost = data.get('total_cost', 0.0)
                analytics.roi = data.get('roi', 0.0)
            
            # Save to database
            if self.supabase:
                await self._save_campaign_analytics_to_database(analytics)
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Campaign analytics update failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def _get_campaign_analytics(self, campaign_id: str) -> Optional[CampaignAnalytics]:
        """Get campaign analytics from database"""
        try:
            if not self.supabase:
                return None
            
            result = self.supabase.table('campaign_analytics').select('*').eq('campaign_id', campaign_id).execute()
            
            if result.data:
                data = result.data[0]
                return CampaignAnalytics(
                    campaign_id=data['campaign_id'],
                    campaign_name=data['campaign_name'],
                    start_date=data['start_date'],
                    end_date=data['end_date'],
                    total_messages_sent=data['total_messages_sent'],
                    successful_deliveries=data['successful_deliveries'],
                    failed_deliveries=data['failed_deliveries'],
                    response_rate=data['response_rate'],
                    engagement_rate=data['engagement_rate'],
                    conversion_rate=data['conversion_rate'],
                    cost_per_message=data['cost_per_message'],
                    total_cost=data['total_cost'],
                    roi=data['roi'],
                    demographics=json.loads(data.get('demographics', '{}')),
                    geographic_distribution=json.loads(data.get('geographic_distribution', '{}')),
                    performance_trends=json.loads(data.get('performance_trends', '[]'))
                )
            
            return None
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Campaign analytics retrieval failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    async def _save_campaign_analytics_to_database(self, analytics: CampaignAnalytics):
        """Save campaign analytics to database"""
        try:
            analytics_data = {
                'campaign_id': analytics.campaign_id,
                'campaign_name': analytics.campaign_name,
                'start_date': analytics.start_date,
                'end_date': analytics.end_date,
                'total_messages_sent': analytics.total_messages_sent,
                'successful_deliveries': analytics.successful_deliveries,
                'failed_deliveries': analytics.failed_deliveries,
                'response_rate': analytics.response_rate,
                'engagement_rate': analytics.engagement_rate,
                'conversion_rate': analytics.conversion_rate,
                'cost_per_message': analytics.cost_per_message,
                'total_cost': analytics.total_cost,
                'roi': analytics.roi,
                'demographics': json.dumps(analytics.demographics),
                'geographic_distribution': json.dumps(analytics.geographic_distribution),
                'performance_trends': json.dumps(analytics.performance_trends),
                'updated_at': datetime.now().isoformat()
            }
            
            # Upsert analytics
            result = self.supabase.table('campaign_analytics').upsert(analytics_data).execute()
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Campaign analytics database saving failed: {str(e)}")
```
