I implemented advanced reporting and visualization capabilities:

```python
class AdvancedReportGenerator:
    def __init__(self, analytics_engine: AdvancedAnalyticsEngine):
        self.analytics_engine = analytics_engine
        self.report_templates = {}
        self.visualization_engines = {}
        
        # Initialize report generation
        self._initialize_report_generation()
    
    def _initialize_report_generation(self):
        """Initialize report generation system"""
        try:
            # Setup report templates
            self._setup_report_templates()
            
            # Setup visualization engines
            self._setup_visualization_engines()
            
            # Setup export formats
            self._setup_export_formats()
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Report generation initialization failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _setup_report_templates(self):
        """Setup report templates"""
        try:
            self.report_templates = {
                'executive_summary': ExecutiveSummaryTemplate(),
                'campaign_performance': CampaignPerformanceTemplate(),
                'user_engagement': UserEngagementTemplate(),
                'system_health': SystemHealthTemplate(),
                'financial_analysis': FinancialAnalysisTemplate(),
                'geographic_insights': GeographicInsightsTemplate()
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Report templates setup failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _setup_visualization_engines(self):
        """Setup visualization engines"""
        try:
            self.visualization_engines = {
                'matplotlib': MatplotlibVisualizationEngine(),
                'plotly': PlotlyVisualizationEngine(),
                'seaborn': SeabornVisualizationEngine(),
                'bokeh': BokehVisualizationEngine()
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Visualization engines setup failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _setup_export_formats(self):
        """Setup export formats"""
        try:
            self.export_formats = {
                'pdf': PDFExporter(),
                'excel': ExcelExporter(),
                'csv': CSVExporter(),
                'html': HTMLExporter(),
                'json': JSONExporter()
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Export formats setup failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def generate_report(self, report_type: ReportType, 
                             start_date: str = None, end_date: str = None,
                             filters: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Generate comprehensive report"""
        try:
            # Get data for report
            report_data = await self._get_report_data(report_type, start_date, end_date, filters)
            
            # Generate report sections
            report_sections = await self._generate_report_sections(report_data, report_type)
            
            # Create visualizations
            visualizations = await self._create_visualizations(report_data, report_type)
            
            # Generate summary
            summary = await self._generate_report_summary(report_data, report_type)
            
            # Compile final report
            final_report = {
                'report_type': report_type.value,
                'generated_at': datetime.now().isoformat(),
                'date_range': {
                    'start_date': start_date,
                    'end_date': end_date
                },
                'summary': summary,
                'sections': report_sections,
                'visualizations': visualizations,
                'metadata': {
                    'total_records': report_data.get('total_records', 0),
                    'data_quality_score': report_data.get('data_quality_score', 0),
                    'generation_time': report_data.get('generation_time', 0)
                }
            }
            
            return final_report
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Report generation failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    async def _get_report_data(self, report_type: ReportType, 
                              start_date: str, end_date: str, 
                              filters: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get data for report"""
        try:
            # Set default date range
            if not start_date:
                start_date = (datetime.now() - timedelta(days=30)).isoformat()
            if not end_date:
                end_date = datetime.now().isoformat()
            
            # Get base data
            base_data = await self._get_base_report_data(start_date, end_date)
            
            # Get specific data based on report type
            if report_type == ReportType.DAILY_SUMMARY:
                specific_data = await self._get_daily_summary_data(start_date, end_date)
            elif report_type == ReportType.WEEKLY_ANALYSIS:
                specific_data = await self._get_weekly_analysis_data(start_date, end_date)
            elif report_type == ReportType.MONTHLY_REPORT:
                specific_data = await self._get_monthly_report_data(start_date, end_date)
            else:
                specific_data = await self._get_custom_range_data(start_date, end_date)
            
            # Apply filters
            if filters:
                specific_data = await self._apply_filters(specific_data, filters)
            
            # Combine data
            report_data = {**base_data, **specific_data}
            
            return report_data
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Report data retrieval failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    async def _get_base_report_data(self, start_date: str, end_date: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get base report data"""
        try:
            # Get campaign data
            campaigns = await self._get_campaigns_data(start_date, end_date)
            
            # Get user data
            users = await self._get_users_data(start_date, end_date)
            
            # Get system data
            system_metrics = await self._get_system_metrics(start_date, end_date)
            
            # Get error data
            errors = await self._get_errors_data(start_date, end_date)
            
            return {
                'campaigns': campaigns,
                'users': users,
                'system_metrics': system_metrics,
                'errors': errors,
                'total_records': len(campaigns) + len(users) + len(system_metrics) + len(errors)
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Base report data retrieval failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    async def _get_campaigns_data(self, start_date: str, end_date: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Get campaigns data"""
        try:
            if not self.analytics_engine.supabase:
                return []
            
            result = self.analytics_engine.supabase.table('campaigns').select('*').gte('created_at', start_date).lte('created_at', end_date).execute()
            
            return result.data or []
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Campaigns data retrieval failed: {str(e)}")
            return []
    
    async def _get_users_data(self, start_date: str, end_date: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Get users data"""
        try:
            if not self.analytics_engine.supabase:
                return []
            
            result = self.analytics_engine.supabase.table('users').select('*').gte('created_at', start_date).lte('created_at', end_date).execute()
            
            return result.data or []
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Users data retrieval failed: {str(e)}")
            return []
    
    async def _get_system_metrics(self, start_date: str, end_date: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Get system metrics data"""
        try:
            if not self.analytics_engine.supabase:
                return []
            
            result = self.analytics_engine.supabase.table('performance_metrics').select('*').gte('timestamp', start_date).lte('timestamp', end_date).execute()
            
            return result.data or []
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ System metrics retrieval failed: {str(e)}")
            return []
    
    async def _get_errors_data(self, start_date: str, end_date: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Get errors data"""
        try:
            if not self.analytics_engine.supabase:
                return []
            
            result = self.analytics_engine.supabase.table('error_logs').select('*').gte('timestamp', start_date).lte('timestamp', end_date).execute()
            
            return result.data or []
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Errors data retrieval failed: {str(e)}")
            return []
    
    async def _generate_report_sections(self, report_data: Dict[str, Any], 
                                       report_type: ReportType) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Generate report sections"""
        try:
            sections = []
            
            # Executive Summary
            executive_summary = await self._generate_executive_summary(report_data)
            sections.append(executive_summary)
            
            # Campaign Performance
            campaign_performance = await self._generate_campaign_performance_section(report_data)
            sections.append(campaign_performance)
            
            # User Engagement
            user_engagement = await self._generate_user_engagement_section(report_data)
            sections.append(user_engagement)
            
            # System Health
            system_health = await self._generate_system_health_section(report_data)
            sections.append(system_health)
            
            # Financial Analysis
            financial_analysis = await self._generate_financial_analysis_section(report_data)
            sections.append(financial_analysis)
            
            # Geographic Insights
            geographic_insights = await self._generate_geographic_insights_section(report_data)
            sections.append(geographic_insights)
            
            return sections
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Report sections generation failed: {str(e)}")
            return []
    
    async def _generate_executive_summary(self, report_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Generate executive summary section"""
        try:
            campaigns = report_data.get('campaigns', [])
            users = report_data.get('users', [])
            system_metrics = report_data.get('system_metrics', [])
            
            # Calculate key metrics
            total_campaigns = len(campaigns)
            total_users = len(users)
            total_messages = sum(campaign.get('total_messages', 0) for campaign in campaigns)
            successful_deliveries = sum(campaign.get('successful_deliveries', 0) for campaign in campaigns)
            total_revenue = sum(campaign.get('revenue', 0) for campaign in campaigns)
            total_cost = sum(campaign.get('total_cost', 0) for campaign in campaigns)
            
            # Calculate rates
            delivery_rate = (successful_deliveries / total_messages * 100) if total_messages > 0 else 0
            roi = ((total_revenue - total_cost) / total_cost * 100) if total_cost > 0 else 0
            
            return {
                'section_name': 'Executive Summary',
                'key_metrics': {
                    'total_campaigns': total_campaigns,
                    'total_users': total_users,
                    'total_messages': total_messages,
                    'successful_deliveries': successful_deliveries,
                    'delivery_rate': round(delivery_rate, 2),
                    'total_revenue': total_revenue,
                    'total_cost': total_cost,
                    'roi': round(roi, 2)
                },
                'insights': [
                    f"Successfully executed {total_campaigns} campaigns",
                    f"Reached {total_users} unique users",
                    f"Achieved {delivery_rate:.1f}% delivery rate",
                    f"Generated {roi:.1f}% ROI"
                ],
                'recommendations': [
                    "Continue current campaign strategies",
                    "Focus on improving delivery rates",
                    "Optimize cost per message",
                    "Expand to new user segments"
                ]
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Executive summary generation failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    async def _create_visualizations(self, report_data: Dict[str, Any], 
                                    report_type: ReportType) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Create visualizations for report"""
        try:
            visualizations = []
            
            # Campaign Performance Chart
            campaign_chart = await self._create_campaign_performance_chart(report_data)
            visualizations.append(campaign_chart)
            
            # User Engagement Chart
            engagement_chart = await self._create_user_engagement_chart(report_data)
            visualizations.append(engagement_chart)
            
            # System Health Chart
            system_chart = await self._create_system_health_chart(report_data)
            visualizations.append(system_chart)
            
            # Financial Analysis Chart
            financial_chart = await self._create_financial_analysis_chart(report_data)
            visualizations.append(financial_chart)
            
            # Geographic Distribution Chart
            geographic_chart = await self._create_geographic_distribution_chart(report_data)
            visualizations.append(geographic_chart)
            
            return visualizations
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Visualizations creation failed: {str(e)}")
            return []
    
    async def _create_campaign_performance_chart(self, report_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Create campaign performance chart"""
        try:
            campaigns = report_data.get('campaigns', [])
            
            # Prepare data
            campaign_names = [campaign.get('name', f"Campaign {i}") for i, campaign in enumerate(campaigns)]
            delivery_rates = [campaign.get('delivery_rate', 0) for campaign in campaigns]
            engagement_rates = [campaign.get('engagement_rate', 0) for campaign in campaigns]
            
            # Create chart
            fig = go.Figure()
            
            # Add delivery rate bar
            fig.add_trace(go.Bar(
                name='Delivery Rate',
                x=campaign_names,
                y=delivery_rates,
                marker_color='lightblue'
            ))
            
            # Add engagement rate bar
            fig.add_trace(go.Bar(
                name='Engagement Rate',
                x=campaign_names,
                y=engagement_rates,
                marker_color='lightgreen'
            ))
            
            # Update layout
            fig.update_layout(
                title='Campaign Performance Comparison',
                xaxis_title='Campaigns',
                yaxis_title='Rate (%)',
                barmode='group',
                height=400
            )
            
            # Convert to HTML
            chart_html = fig.to_html(include_plotlyjs=False, div_id="campaign_performance_chart")
            
            return {
                'chart_name': 'Campaign Performance',
                'chart_type': 'bar',
                'chart_html': chart_html,
                'chart_data': {
                    'campaign_names': campaign_names,
                    'delivery_rates': delivery_rates,
                    'engagement_rates': engagement_rates
                }
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Campaign performance chart creation failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    async def _create_user_engagement_chart(self, report_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Create user engagement chart"""
        try:
            users = report_data.get('users', [])
            
            # Prepare data
            engagement_data = []
            for user in users:
                engagement_data.append({
                    'user_id': user.get('id', ''),
                    'engagement_score': user.get('engagement_score', 0),
                    'last_activity': user.get('last_activity', ''),
                    'total_interactions': user.get('total_interactions', 0)
                })
            
            # Create scatter plot
            fig = go.Figure()
            
            fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                x=[user['total_interactions'] for user in engagement_data],
                y=[user['engagement_score'] for user in engagement_data],
                mode='markers',
                marker=dict(
                    size=10,
                    color=[user['engagement_score'] for user in engagement_data],
                    colorscale='Viridis',
                    showscale=True
                ),
                text=[user['user_id'] for user in engagement_data],
                hovertemplate='User: %{text}<br>Interactions: %{x}<br>Engagement: %{y}<extra></extra>'
            ))
            
            # Update layout
            fig.update_layout(
                title='User Engagement Analysis',
                xaxis_title='Total Interactions',
                yaxis_title='Engagement Score',
                height=400
            )
            
            # Convert to HTML
            chart_html = fig.to_html(include_plotlyjs=False, div_id="user_engagement_chart")
            
            return {
                'chart_name': 'User Engagement',
                'chart_type': 'scatter',
                'chart_html': chart_html,
                'chart_data': engagement_data
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ User engagement chart creation failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    async def _create_system_health_chart(self, report_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Create system health chart"""
        try:
            system_metrics = report_data.get('system_metrics', [])
            
            # Prepare data
            timestamps = []
            cpu_usage = []
            memory_usage = []
            error_rates = []
            
            for metric in system_metrics:
                timestamps.append(metric.get('timestamp', ''))
                cpu_usage.append(metric.get('cpu_usage', 0))
                memory_usage.append(metric.get('memory_usage', 0))
                error_rates.append(metric.get('error_rate', 0))
            
            # Create line chart
            fig = go.Figure()
            
            # Add CPU usage line
            fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                x=timestamps,
                y=cpu_usage,
                mode='lines',
                name='CPU Usage',
                line=dict(color='red')
            ))
            
            # Add memory usage line
            fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                x=timestamps,
                y=memory_usage,
                mode='lines',
                name='Memory Usage',
                line=dict(color='blue')
            ))
            
            # Add error rate line
            fig.add_trace(go.Scatter(
                x=timestamps,
                y=error_rates,
                mode='lines',
                name='Error Rate',
                line=dict(color='orange')
            ))
            
            # Update layout
            fig.update_layout(
                title='System Health Metrics',
                xaxis_title='Time',
                yaxis_title='Usage (%)',
                height=400
            )
            
            # Convert to HTML
            chart_html = fig.to_html(include_plotlyjs=False, div_id="system_health_chart")
            
            return {
                'chart_name': 'System Health',
                'chart_type': 'line',
                'chart_html': chart_html,
                'chart_data': {
                    'timestamps': timestamps,
                    'cpu_usage': cpu_usage,
                    'memory_usage': memory_usage,
                    'error_rates': error_rates
                }
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ System health chart creation failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    async def _create_financial_analysis_chart(self, report_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Create financial analysis chart"""
        try:
            campaigns = report_data.get('campaigns', [])
            
            # Prepare data
            campaign_names = [campaign.get('name', f"Campaign {i}") for i, campaign in enumerate(campaigns)]
            costs = [campaign.get('total_cost', 0) for campaign in campaigns]
            revenues = [campaign.get('revenue', 0) for campaign in campaigns]
            profits = [revenue - cost for revenue, cost in zip(revenues, costs)]
            
            # Create grouped bar chart
            fig = go.Figure()
            
            # Add cost bar
            fig.add_trace(go.Bar(
                name='Cost',
                x=campaign_names,
                y=costs,
                marker_color='red'
            ))
            
            # Add revenue bar
            fig.add_trace(go.Bar(
                name='Revenue',
                x=campaign_names,
                y=revenues,
                marker_color='green'
            ))
            
            # Add profit bar
            fig.add_trace(go.Bar(
                name='Profit',
                x=campaign_names,
                y=profits,
                marker_color='blue'
            ))
            
            # Update layout
            fig.update_layout(
                title='Financial Analysis by Campaign',
                xaxis_title='Campaigns',
                yaxis_title='Amount ($)',
                barmode='group',
                height=400
            )
            
            # Convert to HTML
            chart_html = fig.to_html(include_plotlyjs=False, div_id="financial_analysis_chart")
            
            return {
                'chart_name': 'Financial Analysis',
                'chart_type': 'bar',
                'chart_html': chart_html,
                'chart_data': {
                    'campaign_names': campaign_names,
                    'costs': costs,
                    'revenues': revenues,
                    'profits': profits
                }
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Financial analysis chart creation failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    async def _create_geographic_distribution_chart(self, report_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Create geographic distribution chart"""
        try:
            campaigns = report_data.get('campaigns', [])
            
            # Prepare geographic data
            geographic_data = {}
            for campaign in campaigns:
                geo_dist = campaign.get('geographic_distribution', {})
                for country, count in geo_dist.items():
                    if country not in geographic_data:
                        geographic_data[country] = 0
                    geographic_data[country] += count
            
            # Create pie chart
            countries = list(geographic_data.keys())
            counts = list(geographic_data.values())
            
            fig = go.Figure(data=[go.Pie(
                labels=countries,
                values=counts,
                hole=0.3
            )])
            
            # Update layout
            fig.update_layout(
                title='Geographic Distribution',
                height=400
            )
            
            # Convert to HTML
            chart_html = fig.to_html(include_plotlyjs=False, div_id="geographic_distribution_chart")
            
            return {
                'chart_name': 'Geographic Distribution',
                'chart_type': 'pie',
                'chart_html': chart_html,
                'chart_data': {
                    'countries': countries,
                    'counts': counts
                }
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Geographic distribution chart creation failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    async def export_report(self, report: Dict[str, Any], format: str) -> bytes:
        """Export report in specified format"""
        try:
            if format not in self.export_formats:
                raise ValueError(f"Unsupported export format: {format}")
            
            exporter = self.export_formats[format]
            exported_data = await exporter.export(report)
            
            return exported_data
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Report export failed: {str(e)}")
            return b''
```
