I implemented an advanced machine learning integration system:

```python
import asyncio
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional, Tuple, Union
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
from enum import Enum
import json
import pickle
import joblib
from sklearn.model_selection import train_test_split, cross_val_score, GridSearchCV
from sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier, GradientBoostingRegressor, IsolationForest
from sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression, LinearRegression
from sklearn.cluster import KMeans, DBSCAN
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler, LabelEncoder, OneHotEncoder
from sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score, precision_score, recall_score, f1_score, mean_squared_error, r2_score
from sklearn.feature_extraction.text import TfidfVectorizer, CountVectorizer
from sklearn.decomposition import PCA, TruncatedSVD
import tensorflow as tf
from tensorflow.keras.models import Sequential, Model
from tensorflow.keras.layers import Dense, LSTM, GRU, Embedding, Dropout, BatchNormalization
from tensorflow.keras.optimizers import Adam, RMSprop
from tensorflow.keras.callbacks import EarlyStopping, ReduceLROnPlateau
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.optim as optim
from transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModel, pipeline
import nltk
from nltk.sentiment import SentimentIntensityAnalyzer
from nltk.tokenize import word_tokenize
from nltk.corpus import stopwords
import spacy
from textblob import TextBlob
import cv2
from PIL import Image
import requests
from io import BytesIO

class MLTaskType(Enum):
    CLASSIFICATION = "classification"
    REGRESSION = "regression"
    CLUSTERING = "clustering"
    NLP = "nlp"
    COMPUTER_VISION = "computer_vision"
    TIME_SERIES = "time_series"
    ANOMALY_DETECTION = "anomaly_detection"

class ModelType(Enum):
    SKLEARN = "sklearn"
    TENSORFLOW = "tensorflow"
    PYTORCH = "pytorch"
    TRANSFORMERS = "transformers"
    CUSTOM = "custom"

@dataclass
class MLModel:
    model_id: str
    model_name: str
    model_type: ModelType
    task_type: MLTaskType
    model_object: Any
    training_data: Dict[str, Any]
    performance_metrics: Dict[str, float]
    created_at: str
    updated_at: str
    version: str
    metadata: Dict[str, Any]

@dataclass
class PredictionResult:
    prediction_id: str
    model_id: str
    input_data: Dict[str, Any]
    prediction: Any
    confidence: float
    timestamp: str
    metadata: Dict[str, Any]

@dataclass
class TrainingResult:
    model_id: str
    training_accuracy: float
    validation_accuracy: float
    test_accuracy: float
    training_loss: float
    validation_loss: float
    metrics: Dict[str, float]
    training_time: float
    model_size: int

class AdvancedMLManager:
    def __init__(self, supabase_client, redis_client):
        self.supabase = supabase_client
        self.redis = redis_client
        self.models = {}
        self.predictors = {}
        self.preprocessors = {}
        self.ml_config = {
            'model_storage_path': './models/',
            'cache_models': True,
            'auto_retrain': True,
            'retrain_threshold': 0.1,
            'max_models': 50
        }
        
        # Initialize ML components
        self._initialize_ml_components()
        
        # Load existing models
        self._load_existing_models()
    
    def _initialize_ml_components(self):
        """Initialize ML components"""
        try:
            # Initialize NLP components
            self._initialize_nlp_components()
            
            # Initialize computer vision components
            self._initialize_cv_components()
            
            # Initialize preprocessing components
            self._initialize_preprocessing()
            
            # Initialize model storage
            self._initialize_model_storage()
            
            logger.info("✅ ML components initialized")
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ ML components initialization failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _initialize_nlp_components(self):
        """Initialize NLP components"""
        try:
            # Download NLTK data
            nltk.download('punkt', quiet=True)
            nltk.download('stopwords', quiet=True)
            nltk.download('vader_lexicon', quiet=True)
            
            # Initialize sentiment analyzer
            self.sentiment_analyzer = SentimentIntensityAnalyzer()
            
            # Initialize spaCy model
            try:
                self.nlp = spacy.load("en_core_web_sm")
            except OSError:
                logger.warning("spaCy English model not found, using basic tokenization")
                self.nlp = None
            
            # Initialize transformers pipeline
            try:
                self.sentiment_pipeline = pipeline("sentiment-analysis", 
                                                  model="cardiffnlp/twitter-roberta-base-sentiment-latest")
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"Transformers sentiment model not available: {str(e)}")
                self.sentiment_pipeline = None
            
            # Initialize text vectorizers
            self.tfidf_vectorizer = TfidfVectorizer(max_features=10000, stop_words='english')
            self.count_vectorizer = CountVectorizer(max_features=10000, stop_words='english')
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ NLP components initialization failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _initialize_cv_components(self):
        """Initialize computer vision components"""
        try:
            # Initialize OpenCV
            self.cv2 = cv2
            
            # Initialize PIL
            self.PIL = Image
            
            # Initialize image preprocessing
            self.image_preprocessor = ImagePreprocessor()
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ CV components initialization failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _initialize_preprocessing(self):
        """Initialize preprocessing components"""
        try:
            self.scalers = {
                'standard': StandardScaler(),
                'minmax': None,  # Will be initialized when needed
                'robust': None
            }
            
            self.encoders = {
                'label': LabelEncoder(),
                'onehot': OneHotEncoder(handle_unknown='ignore')
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Preprocessing initialization failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _initialize_model_storage(self):
        """Initialize model storage"""
        try:
            import os
            os.makedirs(self.ml_config['model_storage_path'], exist_ok=True)
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Model storage initialization failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _load_existing_models(self):
        """Load existing models from storage"""
        try:
            if not self.supabase:
                return
            
            result = self.supabase.table('ml_models').select('*').execute()
            
            for model_data in result.data:
                model = MLModel(
                    model_id=model_data['model_id'],
                    model_name=model_data['model_name'],
                    model_type=ModelType(model_data['model_type']),
                    task_type=MLTaskType(model_data['task_type']),
                    model_object=None,  # Will be loaded when needed
                    training_data=json.loads(model_data.get('training_data', '{}')),
                    performance_metrics=json.loads(model_data.get('performance_metrics', '{}')),
                    created_at=model_data['created_at'],
                    updated_at=model_data['updated_at'],
                    version=model_data['version'],
                    metadata=json.loads(model_data.get('metadata', '{}'))
                )
                
                self.models[model.model_id] = model
            
            logger.info(f"✅ Loaded {len(self.models)} existing models")
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Model loading failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def create_model(self, model_name: str, task_type: MLTaskType, 
                          model_type: ModelType, config: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> str:
        """Create new ML model"""
        try:
            # Generate model ID
            model_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
            
            # Create model object based on type
            model_object = await self._create_model_object(task_type, model_type, config)
            
            # Create model record
            model = MLModel(
                model_id=model_id,
                model_name=model_name,
                model_type=model_type,
                task_type=task_type,
                model_object=model_object,
                training_data={},
                performance_metrics={},
                created_at=datetime.now().isoformat(),
                updated_at=datetime.now().isoformat(),
                version='1.0.0',
                metadata=config or {}
            )
            
            # Store model
            self.models[model_id] = model
            
            # Save to database
            if self.supabase:
                await self._save_model_to_database(model)
            
            logger.info(f"✅ Created model: {model_name}")
            return model_id
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Model creation failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    async def _create_model_object(self, task_type: MLTaskType, model_type: ModelType, 
                                  config: Dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
        """Create model object based on type"""
        try:
            if model_type == ModelType.SKLEARN:
                return await self._create_sklearn_model(task_type, config)
            elif model_type == ModelType.TENSORFLOW:
                return await self._create_tensorflow_model(task_type, config)
            elif model_type == ModelType.PYTORCH:
                return await self._create_pytorch_model(task_type, config)
            elif model_type == ModelType.TRANSFORMERS:
                return await self._create_transformers_model(task_type, config)
            else:
                raise ValueError(f"Unsupported model type: {model_type}")
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Model object creation failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    async def _create_sklearn_model(self, task_type: MLTaskType, config: Dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
        """Create sklearn model"""
        try:
            if task_type == MLTaskType.CLASSIFICATION:
                return RandomForestClassifier(
                    n_estimators=config.get('n_estimators', 100),
                    max_depth=config.get('max_depth', None),
                    random_state=42
                )
            elif task_type == MLTaskType.REGRESSION:
                return GradientBoostingRegressor(
                    n_estimators=config.get('n_estimators', 100),
                    learning_rate=config.get('learning_rate', 0.1),
                    random_state=42
                )
            elif task_type == MLTaskType.CLUSTERING:
                return KMeans(
                    n_clusters=config.get('n_clusters', 3),
                    random_state=42
                )
            elif task_type == MLTaskType.ANOMALY_DETECTION:
                return IsolationForest(
                    contamination=config.get('contamination', 0.1),
                    random_state=42
                )
            else:
                raise ValueError(f"Unsupported task type for sklearn: {task_type}")
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Sklearn model creation failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    async def _create_tensorflow_model(self, task_type: MLTaskType, config: Dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
        """Create TensorFlow model"""
        try:
            model = Sequential()
            
            # Input layer
            input_dim = config.get('input_dim', 100)
            model.add(Dense(config.get('hidden_units', 128), 
                          activation='relu', input_dim=input_dim))
            model.add(Dropout(config.get('dropout_rate', 0.2)))
            
            # Hidden layers
            for i in range(config.get('hidden_layers', 2)):
                model.add(Dense(config.get('hidden_units', 128), activation='relu'))
                model.add(BatchNormalization())
                model.add(Dropout(config.get('dropout_rate', 0.2)))
            
            # Output layer
            if task_type == MLTaskType.CLASSIFICATION:
                output_units = config.get('output_units', 2)
                activation = 'softmax' if output_units > 2 else 'sigmoid'
                model.add(Dense(output_units, activation=activation))
                model.compile(optimizer=Adam(learning_rate=config.get('learning_rate', 0.001)),
                            loss='categorical_crossentropy' if output_units > 2 else 'binary_crossentropy',
                            metrics=['accuracy'])
            elif task_type == MLTaskType.REGRESSION:
                model.add(Dense(1, activation='linear'))
                model.compile(optimizer=Adam(learning_rate=config.get('learning_rate', 0.001)),
                            loss='mse', metrics=['mae'])
            
            return model
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ TensorFlow model creation failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    async def _create_pytorch_model(self, task_type: MLTaskType, config: Dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
        """Create PyTorch model"""
        try:
            class MLPModel(nn.Module):
                def __init__(self, input_dim, hidden_dim, output_dim, num_layers=3):
                    super(MLPModel, self).__init__()
                    self.layers = nn.ModuleList()
                    
                    # Input layer
                    self.layers.append(nn.Linear(input_dim, hidden_dim))
                    self.layers.append(nn.ReLU())
                    self.layers.append(nn.Dropout(0.2))
                    
                    # Hidden layers
                    for _ in range(num_layers - 2):
                        self.layers.append(nn.Linear(hidden_dim, hidden_dim))
                        self.layers.append(nn.ReLU())
                        self.layers.append(nn.Dropout(0.2))
                    
                    # Output layer
                    self.layers.append(nn.Linear(hidden_dim, output_dim))
                    
                    if task_type == MLTaskType.CLASSIFICATION:
                        self.layers.append(nn.Softmax(dim=1))
                
                def forward(self, x):
                    for layer in self.layers:
                        x = layer(x)
                    return x
            
            input_dim = config.get('input_dim', 100)
            hidden_dim = config.get('hidden_dim', 128)
            output_dim = config.get('output_dim', 2)
            num_layers = config.get('num_layers', 3)
            
            model = MLPModel(input_dim, hidden_dim, output_dim, num_layers)
            
            # Set optimizer and loss function
            if task_type == MLTaskType.CLASSIFICATION:
                criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()
            else:
                criterion = nn.MSELoss()
            
            optimizer = optim.Adam(model.parameters(), lr=config.get('learning_rate', 0.001))
            
            return {
                'model': model,
                'criterion': criterion,
                'optimizer': optimizer
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ PyTorch model creation failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    async def _create_transformers_model(self, task_type: MLTaskType, config: Dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
        """Create transformers model"""
        try:
            model_name = config.get('model_name', 'bert-base-uncased')
            
            if task_type == MLTaskType.NLP:
                # For text classification
                tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_name)
                model = AutoModel.from_pretrained(model_name)
                
                return {
                    'tokenizer': tokenizer,
                    'model': model,
                    'task_type': 'text_classification'
                }
            else:
                raise ValueError(f"Transformers model not supported for task type: {task_type}")
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Transformers model creation failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    async def train_model(self, model_id: str, training_data: Dict[str, Any], 
                         validation_data: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> TrainingResult:
        """Train ML model"""
        try:
            model = self.models.get(model_id)
            if not model:
                raise ValueError(f"Model {model_id} not found")
            
            # Prepare training data
            X_train, y_train = await self._prepare_training_data(training_data, model.task_type)
            X_val, y_val = None, None
            
            if validation_data:
                X_val, y_val = await self._prepare_training_data(validation_data, model.task_type)
            
            # Train model based on type
            if model.model_type == ModelType.SKLEARN:
                training_result = await self._train_sklearn_model(model, X_train, y_train, X_val, y_val)
            elif model.model_type == ModelType.TENSORFLOW:
                training_result = await self._train_tensorflow_model(model, X_train, y_train, X_val, y_val)
            elif model.model_type == ModelType.PYTORCH:
                training_result = await self._train_pytorch_model(model, X_train, y_train, X_val, y_val)
            else:
                raise ValueError(f"Training not supported for model type: {model.model_type}")
            
            # Update model performance metrics
            model.performance_metrics = training_result.metrics
            model.updated_at = datetime.now().isoformat()
            
            # Save updated model
            if self.supabase:
                await self._update_model_in_database(model)
            
            logger.info(f"✅ Model {model_id} trained successfully")
            return training_result
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Model training failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    async def _prepare_training_data(self, data: Dict[str, Any], task_type: MLTaskType) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
        """Prepare training data"""
        try:
            # Extract features and labels
            X = np.array(data.get('features', []))
            y = np.array(data.get('labels', []))
            
            # Preprocess based on task type
            if task_type == MLTaskType.CLASSIFICATION:
                # Encode labels if they are strings
                if y.dtype == object:
                    le = LabelEncoder()
                    y = le.fit_transform(y)
            elif task_type == MLTaskType.REGRESSION:
                # Ensure labels are numeric
                y = y.astype(float)
            
            # Scale features
            scaler = StandardScaler()
            X = scaler.fit_transform(X)
            
            return X, y
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Training data preparation failed: {str(e)}")
            return None, None
    
    async def _train_sklearn_model(self, model: MLModel, X_train: np.ndarray, y_train: np.ndarray, 
                                  X_val: np.ndarray, y_val: np.ndarray) -> TrainingResult:
        """Train sklearn model"""
        try:
            start_time = time.time()
            
            # Train model
            model.model_object.fit(X_train, y_train)
            
            # Make predictions
            y_pred_train = model.model_object.predict(X_train)
            y_pred_val = model.model_object.predict(X_val) if X_val is not None else None
            
            # Calculate metrics
            train_accuracy = accuracy_score(y_train, y_pred_train) if model.task_type == MLTaskType.CLASSIFICATION else r2_score(y_train, y_pred_train)
            val_accuracy = accuracy_score(y_val, y_pred_val) if X_val is not None and model.task_type == MLTaskType.CLASSIFICATION else None
            
            if model.task_type == MLTaskType.CLASSIFICATION:
                precision = precision_score(y_train, y_pred_train, average='weighted')
                recall = recall_score(y_train, y_pred_train, average='weighted')
                f1 = f1_score(y_train, y_pred_train, average='weighted')
            else:
                precision = recall = f1 = None
            
            training_time = time.time() - start_time
            
            return TrainingResult(
                model_id=model.model_id,
                training_accuracy=train_accuracy,
                validation_accuracy=val_accuracy,
                test_accuracy=None,
                training_loss=None,
                validation_loss=None,
                metrics={
                    'accuracy': train_accuracy,
                    'precision': precision,
                    'recall': recall,
                    'f1_score': f1
                },
                training_time=training_time,
                model_size=len(pickle.dumps(model.model_object))
            )
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Sklearn model training failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    async def _train_tensorflow_model(self, model: MLModel, X_train: np.ndarray, y_train: np.ndarray, 
                                     X_val: np.ndarray, y_val: np.ndarray) -> TrainingResult:
        """Train TensorFlow model"""
        try:
            start_time = time.time()
            
            # Prepare data
            if model.task_type == MLTaskType.CLASSIFICATION:
                # Convert labels to categorical if multi-class
                if len(np.unique(y_train)) > 2:
                    y_train = tf.keras.utils.to_categorical(y_train)
                    if y_val is not None:
                        y_val = tf.keras.utils.to_categorical(y_val)
            
            # Set up callbacks
            callbacks = [
                EarlyStopping(patience=10, restore_best_weights=True),
                ReduceLROnPlateau(factor=0.5, patience=5)
            ]
            
            # Train model
            history = model.model_object.fit(
                X_train, y_train,
                validation_data=(X_val, y_val) if X_val is not None else None,
                epochs=100,
                batch_size=32,
                callbacks=callbacks,
                verbose=0
            )
            
            # Calculate metrics
            train_loss = history.history['loss'][-1]
            val_loss = history.history.get('val_loss', [None])[-1]
            train_accuracy = history.history['accuracy'][-1]
            val_accuracy = history.history.get('val_accuracy', [None])[-1]
            
            training_time = time.time() - start_time
            
            return TrainingResult(
                model_id=model.model_id,
                training_accuracy=train_accuracy,
                validation_accuracy=val_accuracy,
                test_accuracy=None,
                training_loss=train_loss,
                validation_loss=val_loss,
                metrics={
                    'accuracy': train_accuracy,
                    'loss': train_loss
                },
                training_time=training_time,
                model_size=len(model.model_object.to_json())
            )
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ TensorFlow model training failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    async def _train_pytorch_model(self, model: MLModel, X_train: np.ndarray, y_train: np.ndarray, 
                                  X_val: np.ndarray, y_val: np.ndarray) -> TrainingResult:
        """Train PyTorch model"""
        try:
            start_time = time.time()
            
            # Convert to PyTorch tensors
            X_train_tensor = torch.FloatTensor(X_train)
            y_train_tensor = torch.LongTensor(y_train) if model.task_type == MLTaskType.CLASSIFICATION else torch.FloatTensor(y_train)
            
            if X_val is not None:
                X_val_tensor = torch.FloatTensor(X_val)
                y_val_tensor = torch.LongTensor(y_val) if model.task_type == MLTaskType.CLASSIFICATION else torch.FloatTensor(y_val)
            
            # Get model components
            model_obj = model.model_object['model']
            criterion = model.model_object['criterion']
            optimizer = model.model_object['optimizer']
            
            # Training loop
            model_obj.train()
            for epoch in range(100):
                optimizer.zero_grad()
                outputs = model_obj(X_train_tensor)
                loss = criterion(outputs, y_train_tensor)
                loss.backward()
                optimizer.step()
                
                if epoch % 10 == 0:
                    logger.info(f"Epoch {epoch}, Loss: {loss.item()}")
            
            # Calculate final metrics
            model_obj.eval()
            with torch.no_grad():
                train_pred = model_obj(X_train_tensor)
                train_accuracy = accuracy_score(y_train, train_pred.argmax(dim=1)) if model.task_type == MLTaskType.CLASSIFICATION else r2_score(y_train, train_pred.numpy())
                
                if X_val is not None:
                    val_pred = model_obj(X_val_tensor)
                    val_accuracy = accuracy_score(y_val, val_pred.argmax(dim=1)) if model.task_type == MLTaskType.CLASSIFICATION else r2_score(y_val, val_pred.numpy())
                else:
                    val_accuracy = None
            
            training_time = time.time() - start_time
            
            return TrainingResult(
                model_id=model.model_id,
                training_accuracy=train_accuracy,
                validation_accuracy=val_accuracy,
                test_accuracy=None,
                training_loss=loss.item(),
                validation_loss=None,
                metrics={
                    'accuracy': train_accuracy,
                    'loss': loss.item()
                },
                training_time=training_time,
                model_size=len(pickle.dumps(model_obj))
            )
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ PyTorch model training failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    async def predict(self, model_id: str, input_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> PredictionResult:
        """Make prediction using trained model"""
        try:
            model = self.models.get(model_id)
            if not model:
                raise ValueError(f"Model {model_id} not found")
            
            # Prepare input data
            X = await self._prepare_prediction_data(input_data, model.task_type)
            
            # Make prediction based on model type
            if model.model_type == ModelType.SKLEARN:
                prediction = model.model_object.predict(X)
                confidence = model.model_object.predict_proba(X).max() if hasattr(model.model_object, 'predict_proba') else 1.0
            elif model.model_type == ModelType.TENSORFLOW:
                prediction = model.model_object.predict(X)
                confidence = prediction.max() if model.task_type == MLTaskType.CLASSIFICATION else 1.0
            elif model.model_type == ModelType.PYTORCH:
                X_tensor = torch.FloatTensor(X)
                model_obj = model.model_object['model']
                model_obj.eval()
                with torch.no_grad():
                    prediction = model_obj(X_tensor)
                    confidence = prediction.max().item() if model.task_type == MLTaskType.CLASSIFICATION else 1.0
            else:
                raise ValueError(f"Prediction not supported for model type: {model.model_type}")
            
            # Create prediction result
            result = PredictionResult(
                prediction_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
                model_id=model_id,
                input_data=input_data,
                prediction=prediction.tolist() if hasattr(prediction, 'tolist') else prediction,
                confidence=float(confidence),
                timestamp=datetime.now().isoformat(),
                metadata={}
            )
            
            return result
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Prediction failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    async def _prepare_prediction_data(self, data: Dict[str, Any], task_type: MLTaskType) -> np.ndarray:
        """Prepare prediction data"""
        try:
            # Extract features
            X = np.array(data.get('features', []))
            
            # Preprocess based on task type
            if task_type == MLTaskType.CLASSIFICATION:
                # Scale features
                scaler = StandardScaler()
                X = scaler.fit_transform(X)
            elif task_type == MLTaskType.REGRESSION:
                # Scale features
                scaler = StandardScaler()
                X = scaler.fit_transform(X)
            
            return X
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Prediction data preparation failed: {str(e)}")
            return None
    
    async def analyze_text_sentiment(self, text: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Analyze text sentiment using multiple methods"""
        try:
            results = {}
            
            # NLTK VADER sentiment
            vader_scores = self.sentiment_analyzer.polarity_scores(text)
            results['vader'] = {
                'compound': vader_scores['compound'],
                'positive': vader_scores['pos'],
                'negative': vader_scores['neg'],
                'neutral': vader_scores['neu']
            }
            
            # TextBlob sentiment
            blob = TextBlob(text)
            results['textblob'] = {
                'polarity': blob.sentiment.polarity,
                'subjectivity': blob.sentiment.subjectivity
            }
            
            # Transformers sentiment (if available)
            if self.sentiment_pipeline:
                try:
                    transformer_result = self.sentiment_pipeline(text)
                    results['transformer'] = transformer_result[0]
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.warning(f"Transformer sentiment analysis failed: {str(e)}")
            
            # Determine overall sentiment
            overall_sentiment = self._determine_overall_sentiment(results)
            results['overall'] = overall_sentiment
            
            return results
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Text sentiment analysis failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    def _determine_overall_sentiment(self, results: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        """Determine overall sentiment from multiple methods"""
        try:
            # Use VADER compound score as primary indicator
            vader_compound = results.get('vader', {}).get('compound', 0)
            textblob_polarity = results.get('textblob', {}).get('polarity', 0)
            
            # Combine scores
            combined_score = (vader_compound + textblob_polarity) / 2
            
            if combined_score > 0.1:
                return 'positive'
            elif combined_score < -0.1:
                return 'negative'
            else:
                return 'neutral'
                
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Overall sentiment determination failed: {str(e)}")
            return 'neutral'
    
    async def extract_text_features(self, text: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Extract features from text"""
        try:
            features = {}
            
            # Basic text features
            features['length'] = len(text)
            features['word_count'] = len(text.split())
            features['sentence_count'] = len(text.split('.'))
            features['avg_word_length'] = np.mean([len(word) for word in text.split()])
            
            # N-gram features
            words = text.lower().split()
            features['bigrams'] = len(list(zip(words, words[1:])))
            features['trigrams'] = len(list(zip(words, words[1:], words[2:])))
            
            # TF-IDF features
            tfidf_features = self.tfidf_vectorizer.fit_transform([text])
            features['tfidf_sum'] = tfidf_features.sum()
            features['tfidf_mean'] = tfidf_features.mean()
            
            # Sentiment features
            sentiment = await self.analyze_text_sentiment(text)
            features['sentiment_positive'] = sentiment.get('vader', {}).get('positive', 0)
            features['sentiment_negative'] = sentiment.get('vader', {}).get('negative', 0)
            features['sentiment_neutral'] = sentiment.get('vader', {}).get('neutral', 0)
            
            return features
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Text feature extraction failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    async def analyze_image_content(self, image_url: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Analyze image content"""
        try:
            # Download image
            response = requests.get(image_url)
            image = Image.open(BytesIO(response.content))
            
            # Convert to OpenCV format
            cv_image = cv2.cvtColor(np.array(image), cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR)
            
            # Extract features
            features = {}
            
            # Basic image properties
            features['width'] = image.width
            features['height'] = image.height
            features['aspect_ratio'] = image.width / image.height
            features['total_pixels'] = image.width * image.height
            
            # Color analysis
            features['dominant_colors'] = self._extract_dominant_colors(cv_image)
            features['brightness'] = np.mean(cv_image)
            features['contrast'] = np.std(cv_image)
            
            # Edge detection
            gray = cv2.cvtColor(cv_image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
            edges = cv2.Canny(gray, 50, 150)
            features['edge_density'] = np.sum(edges > 0) / (image.width * image.height)
            
            # Texture analysis
            features['texture_energy'] = self._calculate_texture_energy(gray)
            
            return features
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Image content analysis failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    def _extract_dominant_colors(self, image: np.ndarray, k: int = 5) -> List[Tuple[int, int, int]]:
        """Extract dominant colors from image"""
        try:
            # Reshape image
            data = image.reshape((-1, 3))
            data = np.float32(data)
            
            # Apply K-means clustering
            criteria = (cv2.TERM_CRITERIA_EPS + cv2.TERM_CRITERIA_MAX_ITER, 20, 1.0)
            _, labels, centers = cv2.kmeans(data, k, None, criteria, 10, cv2.KMEANS_RANDOM_CENTERS)
            
            # Convert centers to integers
            centers = np.uint8(centers)
            
            return [tuple(color) for color in centers]
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Dominant color extraction failed: {str(e)}")
            return []
    
    def _calculate_texture_energy(self, gray_image: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """Calculate texture energy using local binary patterns"""
        try:
            # Simple texture energy calculation
            # This is a simplified version - in practice, you'd use more sophisticated methods
            sobelx = cv2.Sobel(gray_image, cv2.CV_64F, 1, 0, ksize=3)
            sobely = cv2.Sobel(gray_image, cv2.CV_64F, 0, 1, ksize=3)
            
            energy = np.sqrt(sobelx**2 + sobely**2)
            return np.mean(energy)
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Texture energy calculation failed: {str(e)}")
            return 0.0
    
    async def detect_anomalies(self, data: List[Dict[str, Any]], 
                              features: List[str]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Detect anomalies in data"""
        try:
            # Prepare data
            X = np.array([[item.get(feature, 0) for feature in features] for item in data])
            
            # Create anomaly detector
            detector = IsolationForest(contamination=0.1, random_state=42)
            anomaly_labels = detector.fit_predict(X)
            anomaly_scores = detector.decision_function(X)
            
            # Add anomaly information to data
            results = []
            for i, item in enumerate(data):
                item_copy = item.copy()
                item_copy['is_anomaly'] = anomaly_labels[i] == -1
                item_copy['anomaly_score'] = anomaly_scores[i]
                results.append(item_copy)
            
            return results
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Anomaly detection failed: {str(e)}")
            return data
    
    async def cluster_data(self, data: List[Dict[str, Any]], 
                          features: List[str], n_clusters: int = 3) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Cluster data using K-means"""
        try:
            # Prepare data
            X = np.array([[item.get(feature, 0) for feature in features] for item in data])
            
            # Apply K-means clustering
            kmeans = KMeans(n_clusters=n_clusters, random_state=42)
            cluster_labels = kmeans.fit_predict(X)
            
            # Add cluster information to data
            results = []
            for i, item in enumerate(data):
                item_copy = item.copy()
                item_copy['cluster'] = int(cluster_labels[i])
                item_copy['cluster_center_distance'] = float(np.linalg.norm(X[i] - kmeans.cluster_centers_[cluster_labels[i]]))
                results.append(item_copy)
            
            return results
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Data clustering failed: {str(e)}")
            return data
    
    async def _save_model_to_database(self, model: MLModel):
        """Save model to database"""
        try:
            model_data = {
                'model_id': model.model_id,
                'model_name': model.model_name,
                'model_type': model.model_type.value,
                'task_type': model.task_type.value,
                'training_data': json.dumps(model.training_data),
                'performance_metrics': json.dumps(model.performance_metrics),
                'created_at': model.created_at,
                'updated_at': model.updated_at,
                'version': model.version,
                'metadata': json.dumps(model.metadata)
            }
            
            result = self.supabase.table('ml_models').upsert(model_data).execute()
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Model database saving failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def _update_model_in_database(self, model: MLModel):
        """Update model in database"""
        try:
            model_data = {
                'model_id': model.model_id,
                'performance_metrics': json.dumps(model.performance_metrics),
                'updated_at': model.updated_at
            }
            
            result = self.supabase.table('ml_models').update(model_data).eq('model_id', model.model_id).execute()
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Model database update failed: {str(e)}")
```

**Afternoon Session (4 hours):**
I implemented predictive analytics and intelligent automation:

```python
class PredictiveAnalyticsEngine:
    def __init__(self, ml_manager: AdvancedMLManager):
        self.ml_manager = ml_manager
        self.prediction_models = {}
        self.automation_rules = {}
        
        # Initialize predictive analytics
        self._initialize_predictive_analytics()
    
    def _initialize_predictive_analytics(self):
        """Initialize predictive analytics engine"""
        try:
            # Setup prediction models
            self._setup_prediction_models()
            
            # Setup automation rules
            self._setup_automation_rules()
            
            # Initialize prediction cache
            self._initialize_prediction_cache()
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Predictive analytics initialization failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _setup_prediction_models(self):
        """Setup prediction models"""
        try:
            self.prediction_models = {
                'user_engagement': None,
                'campaign_success': None,
                'message_response': None,
                'content_performance': None,
                'user_churn': None,
                'optimal_timing': None
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Prediction models setup failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _setup_automation_rules(self):
        """Setup automation rules"""
        try:
            self.automation_rules = {
                'auto_optimize_campaigns': True,
                'auto_adjust_timing': True,
                'auto_personalize_messages': True,
                'auto_detect_anomalies': True,
                'auto_retrain_models': True
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Automation rules setup failed: {str(e)}")
    
    def _initialize_prediction_cache(self):
        """Initialize prediction cache"""
        try:
            self.prediction_cache = {}
            self.cache_ttl = 3600  # 1 hour
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Prediction cache initialization failed: {str(e)}")
    
    async def predict_user_engagement(self, user_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Predict user engagement level"""
        try:
            # Check cache first
            cache_key = f"engagement_{hash(str(user_data))}"
            if cache_key in self.prediction_cache:
                cached_result = self.prediction_cache[cache_key]
                if datetime.now() - cached_result['timestamp'] < timedelta(seconds=self.cache_ttl):
                    return cached_result['prediction']
            
            # Prepare features
            features = await self._prepare_user_engagement_features(user_data)
            
            # Make prediction
            if self.prediction_models['user_engagement']:
                prediction = await self.ml_manager.predict(
                    self.prediction_models['user_engagement'], 
                    {'features': features}
                )
                
                result = {
                    'engagement_level': prediction.prediction[0] if isinstance(prediction.prediction, list) else prediction.prediction,
                    'confidence': prediction.confidence,
                    'recommendations': await self._generate_engagement_recommendations(features, prediction.prediction)
                }
            else:
                # Fallback to rule-based prediction
                result = await self._rule_based_engagement_prediction(user_data)
            
            # Cache result
            self.prediction_cache[cache_key] = {
                'prediction': result,
                'timestamp': datetime.now()
            }
            
            return result
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ User engagement prediction failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    async def _prepare_user_engagement_features(self, user_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[float]:
        """Prepare features for user engagement prediction"""
        try:
            features = []
            
            # Basic user features
            features.append(user_data.get('followers_count', 0))
            features.append(user_data.get('following_count', 0))
            features.append(user_data.get('posts_count', 0))
            features.append(user_data.get('is_verified', 0))
            
            # Activity features
            features.append(user_data.get('avg_likes_per_post', 0))
            features.append(user_data.get('avg_comments_per_post', 0))
            features.append(user_data.get('avg_shares_per_post', 0))
            
            # Engagement rate
            total_posts = user_data.get('posts_count', 1)
            total_likes = user_data.get('total_likes', 0)
            engagement_rate = total_likes / total_posts if total_posts > 0 else 0
            features.append(engagement_rate)
            
            # Account age (if available)
            account_created = user_data.get('account_created_at')
            if account_created:
                account_age_days = (datetime.now() - datetime.fromisoformat(account_created)).days
                features.append(account_age_days)
            else:
                features.append(0)
            
            # Bio features
            bio = user_data.get('bio', '')
            features.append(len(bio))
            features.append(bio.count('#'))  # Hashtag count
            features.append(bio.count('@'))  # Mention count
            
            return features
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ User engagement features preparation failed: {str(e)}")
            return []
    
    async def _generate_engagement_recommendations(self, features: List[float], 
                                                  prediction: Any) -> List[str]:
        """Generate engagement recommendations"""
        try:
            recommendations = []
            
            # Analyze features and provide recommendations
            if features[0] < 1000:  # Low follower count
                recommendations.append("Focus on creating high-quality content to attract more followers")
            
            if features[6] < 0.05:  # Low engagement rate
                recommendations.append("Improve engagement by responding to comments and using relevant hashtags")
            
            if features[8] < 50:  # Short bio
                recommendations.append("Expand your bio to provide more information about yourself")
            
            if features[9] < 3:  # Few hashtags
                recommendations.append("Use more hashtags to increase discoverability")
            
            return recommendations
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Engagement recommendations generation failed: {str(e)}")
            return []
    
    async def _rule_based_engagement_prediction(self, user_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Fallback rule-based engagement prediction"""
        try:
            # Simple rule-based prediction
            followers = user_data.get('followers_count', 0)
            posts = user_data.get('posts_count', 1)
            avg_likes = user_data.get('avg_likes_per_post', 0)
            
            # Calculate engagement score
            engagement_score = (avg_likes / followers * 100) if followers > 0 else 0
            
            # Determine engagement level
            if engagement_score > 5:
                engagement_level = 'high'
            elif engagement_score > 2:
                engagement_level = 'medium'
            else:
                engagement_level = 'low'
            
            return {
                'engagement_level': engagement_level,
                'confidence': 0.7,
                'recommendations': [
                    "This is a rule-based prediction. Train ML models for better accuracy."
                ]
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Rule-based engagement prediction failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    async def predict_campaign_success(self, campaign_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Predict campaign success probability"""
        try:
            # Prepare features
            features = await self._prepare_campaign_features(campaign_data)
            
            # Make prediction
            if self.prediction_models['campaign_success']:
                prediction = await self.ml_manager.predict(
                    self.prediction_models['campaign_success'],
                    {'features': features}
                )
                
                success_probability = prediction.prediction[0] if isinstance(prediction.prediction, list) else prediction.prediction
                
                result = {
                    'success_probability': float(success_probability),
                    'confidence': prediction.confidence,
                    'risk_factors': await self._identify_risk_factors(campaign_data),
                    'optimization_suggestions': await self._generate_optimization_suggestions(campaign_data, success_probability)
                }
            else:
                # Fallback to rule-based prediction
                result = await self._rule_based_campaign_prediction(campaign_data)
            
            return result
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Campaign success prediction failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    async def _prepare_campaign_features(self, campaign_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[float]:
        """Prepare features for campaign success prediction"""
        try:
            features = []
            
            # Campaign basic features
            features.append(campaign_data.get('target_audience_size', 0))
            features.append(campaign_data.get('message_length', 0))
            features.append(campaign_data.get('has_images', 0))
            features.append(campaign_data.get('has_hashtags', 0))
            features.append(campaign_data.get('has_links', 0))
            
            # Timing features
            scheduled_time = campaign_data.get('scheduled_time')
            if scheduled_time:
                hour = datetime.fromisoformat(scheduled_time).hour
                features.append(hour)
                features.append(1 if 9 <= hour <= 17 else 0)  # Business hours
            else:
                features.extend([12, 1])  # Default values
            
            # Content features
            message_text = campaign_data.get('message_text', '')
            features.append(len(message_text))
            features.append(message_text.count('!'))
            features.append(message_text.count('?'))
            
            # Historical performance (if available)
            features.append(campaign_data.get('avg_previous_success_rate', 0.5))
            features.append(campaign_data.get('avg_previous_engagement_rate', 0.1))
            
            return features
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Campaign features preparation failed: {str(e)}")
            return []
    
    async def _identify_risk_factors(self, campaign_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[str]:
        """Identify potential risk factors for campaign"""
        try:
            risk_factors = []
            
            # Check message length
            message_length = campaign_data.get('message_length', 0)
            if message_length > 200:
                risk_factors.append("Message is too long, may reduce engagement")
            elif message_length < 20:
                risk_factors.append("Message is too short, may not be engaging")
            
            # Check timing
            scheduled_time = campaign_data.get('scheduled_time')
            if scheduled_time:
                hour = datetime.fromisoformat(scheduled_time).hour
                if hour < 6 or hour > 22:
                    risk_factors.append("Scheduled time is outside optimal hours")
            
            # Check content
            message_text = campaign_data.get('message_text', '')
            if not message_text:
                risk_factors.append("No message text provided")
            
            if message_text.count('!') > 3:
                risk_factors.append("Too many exclamation marks may seem spammy")
            
            return risk_factors
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Risk factors identification failed: {str(e)}")
            return []
    
    async def _generate_optimization_suggestions(self, campaign_data: Dict[str, Any], 
                                               success_probability: float) -> List[str]:
        """Generate optimization suggestions for campaign"""
        try:
            suggestions = []
            
            if success_probability < 0.5:
                suggestions.append("Consider reducing target audience size for better focus")
                suggestions.append("Add compelling call-to-action to message")
                suggestions.append("Include relevant hashtags")
            
            if success_probability < 0.7:
                suggestions.append("Optimize message timing for target audience")
                suggestions.append("A/B test different message variations")
                suggestions.append("Consider adding visual content")
            
            return suggestions
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Optimization suggestions generation failed: {str(e)}")
            return []
    
    async def predict_optimal_timing(self, user_data: Dict[str, Any], 
                                   campaign_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Predict optimal timing for campaign"""
        try:
            # Analyze user activity patterns
            activity_patterns = await self._analyze_user_activity_patterns(user_data)
            
            # Consider campaign type
            campaign_type = campaign_data.get('type', 'general')
            
            # Predict optimal timing
            optimal_times = await self._calculate_optimal_times(activity_patterns, campaign_type)
            
            return {
                'optimal_hours': optimal_times['hours'],
                'optimal_days': optimal_times['days'],
                'confidence': optimal_times['confidence'],
                'reasoning': optimal_times['reasoning']
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Optimal timing prediction failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    async def _analyze_user_activity_patterns(self, user_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Analyze user activity patterns"""
        try:
            # This would typically analyze historical activity data
            # For now, we'll use simplified patterns
            
            patterns = {
                'peak_hours': [9, 12, 18, 21],  # Typical peak hours
                'peak_days': [1, 2, 3, 4, 5],   # Weekdays
                'activity_level': 'medium',
                'timezone': user_data.get('timezone', 'UTC')
            }
            
            return patterns
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ User activity patterns analysis failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    async def _calculate_optimal_times(self, activity_patterns: Dict[str, Any], 
                                     campaign_type: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Calculate optimal times for campaign"""
        try:
            # Base optimal times
            optimal_hours = activity_patterns.get('peak_hours', [9, 12, 18, 21])
            optimal_days = activity_patterns.get('peak_days', [1, 2, 3, 4, 5])
            
            # Adjust based on campaign type
            if campaign_type == 'business':
                optimal_hours = [9, 10, 11, 14, 15, 16]  # Business hours
            elif campaign_type == 'entertainment':
                optimal_hours = [18, 19, 20, 21, 22]  # Evening hours
            
            return {
                'hours': optimal_hours,
                'days': optimal_days,
                'confidence': 0.8,
                'reasoning': f"Based on {campaign_type} campaign type and user activity patterns"
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Optimal times calculation failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    async def auto_optimize_campaign(self, campaign_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Automatically optimize campaign based on predictions"""
        try:
            # Get campaign data
            campaign_data = await self._get_campaign_data(campaign_id)
            if not campaign_data:
                return {}
            
            # Get optimization suggestions
            success_prediction = await self.predict_campaign_success(campaign_data)
            timing_prediction = await self.predict_optimal_timing({}, campaign_data)
            
            # Generate optimization recommendations
            optimizations = []
            
            # Timing optimization
            if timing_prediction.get('optimal_hours'):
                optimizations.append({
                    'type': 'timing',
                    'suggestion': f"Schedule campaign during hours: {timing_prediction['optimal_hours']}",
                    'impact': 'high'
                })
            
            # Content optimization
            if success_prediction.get('success_probability', 0) < 0.7:
                optimizations.append({
                    'type': 'content',
                    'suggestion': 'Consider adding more engaging content or call-to-action',
                    'impact': 'medium'
                })
            
            # Target audience optimization
            if campaign_data.get('target_audience_size', 0) > 10000:
                optimizations.append({
                    'type': 'audience',
                    'suggestion': 'Consider narrowing target audience for better engagement',
                    'impact': 'high'
                })
            
            return {
                'campaign_id': campaign_id,
                'optimizations': optimizations,
                'predicted_success_rate': success_prediction.get('success_probability', 0),
                'confidence': success_prediction.get('confidence', 0)
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Campaign auto-optimization failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
    
    async def _get_campaign_data(self, campaign_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get campaign data"""
        try:
            # This would typically fetch from database
            # For now, return mock data
            return {
                'id': campaign_id,
                'target_audience_size': 5000,
                'message_text': 'Check out our new product!',
                'message_length': 30,
                'has_images': True,
                'has_hashtags': True,
                'type': 'business'
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"❌ Campaign data retrieval failed: {str(e)}")
            return {}
```

APPENDIX

A. Technical Specifications and Architecture

**A.1 System Architecture Overview**
```
┌─────────────────┐    ┌─────────────────┐    ┌─────────────────┐
│   React Frontend │    │  FastAPI Backend │    │  Supabase DB    │
│   (TypeScript)   │◄──►│    (Python)      │◄──►│  (PostgreSQL)   │
└─────────────────┘    └─────────────────┘    └─────────────────┘
         │                       │                       │
         │                       │                       │
         ▼                       ▼                       ▼
┌─────────────────┐    ┌─────────────────┐    ┌─────────────────┐
│   Ant Design    │    │   Selenium      │    │   External      │
│   UI Components │    │   WebDriver     │    │   APIs          │
└─────────────────┘    └─────────────────┘    └─────────────────┘
                                │                       │
                                │                       │
                                ▼                       ▼
                    ┌─────────────────┐    ┌─────────────────┐
                    │   Apify API     │    │  OpenRouter API │
                    │   (Scraping)    │    │  (AI/ML)        │
                    └─────────────────┘    └─────────────────┘
```

**A.2 Technology Stack**
- **Backend Framework:** FastAPI (Python 3.11)
- **Frontend Framework:** React 18 with TypeScript
- **Database:** Supabase (PostgreSQL)
- **Automation:** Selenium WebDriver, Apify API
- **AI/ML Services:** OpenRouter API, DeepL API
- **UI Framework:** Ant Design
- **Deployment:** Docker, Nginx
- **Version Control:** Git
- **Authentication:** JWT Tokens
- **Caching:** Redis (Optional)

**A.3 System Requirements**
- **Minimum RAM:** 4GB
- **Recommended RAM:** 8GB+
- **Storage:** 20GB+ SSD
- **CPU:** 2+ cores
- **Network:** Stable internet connection
- **Browser:** Chrome/Chromium (for Selenium)

B. Database Schema and Data Models

**B.1 Database Tables Structure**

**Table: leads**
```sql
CREATE TABLE leads (
    id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    username VARCHAR(255) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    full_name VARCHAR(255),
    bio TEXT,
    followers_count INTEGER,
    following_count INTEGER,
    posts_count INTEGER,
    is_private BOOLEAN,
    is_verified BOOLEAN,
    profile_pic_url TEXT,
    nationality VARCHAR(100),
    session_id VARCHAR(255),
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT NOW(),
    last_updated TIMESTAMP DEFAULT NOW()
);
```

**Table: sessions**
```sql
CREATE TABLE sessions (
    id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    session_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    session_type VARCHAR(100),
    status VARCHAR(50),
    total_profiles INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    processed_profiles INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT NOW()
);
```

**Table: instagram_accounts**
```sql
CREATE TABLE instagram_accounts (
    id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    username VARCHAR(255) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    password VARCHAR(255),
    email VARCHAR(255),
    status VARCHAR(50),
    last_used TIMESTAMP,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT NOW()
);
```

**Table: message_templates**
```sql
CREATE TABLE message_templates (
    id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    template_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    template_content TEXT NOT NULL,
    language VARCHAR(10),
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT NOW()
);
```

**Table: campaigns**
```sql
CREATE TABLE campaigns (
    id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    campaign_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    template_id INTEGER REFERENCES message_templates(id),
    target_leads TEXT[],
    status VARCHAR(50),
    sent_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    success_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT NOW()
);
```

**B.2 Data Flow Diagram**
```
User Input → Frontend → API Endpoints → Backend Processing → Database
    ↓              ↓           ↓              ↓              ↓
Profile URLs → React UI → FastAPI → Selenium/Apify → Supabase
    ↓              ↓           ↓              ↓              ↓
Campaign Data → Form Validation → AI Classification → Data Storage
    ↓              ↓           ↓              ↓              ↓
Message Templates → Translation → Campaign Execution → Results
```

C. API Endpoints Documentation

**C.1 Profile Scraping Endpoints**
```
POST /campaigns/profile-scraping
- Description: Scrape Instagram profiles
- Parameters: { "usernames": ["user1", "user2"], "session_name": "session1" }
- Response: { "success": true, "session_id": "uuid", "profiles": [...] }
```

**C.2 Nationality Classification Endpoints**
```
POST /campaigns/nationality-classification
- Description: Classify user nationalities using AI
- Parameters: { "session_id": "uuid", "use_existing_data": true }
- Response: { "success": true, "classified_count": 150, "results": [...] }
```

**C.3 Message Campaign Endpoints**
```
POST /campaigns/message-campaign
- Description: Send messages to classified users
- Parameters: { "template_id": 1, "target_leads": ["user1", "user2"] }
- Response: { "success": true, "sent_count": 50, "results": [...] }
```

**C.4 Data Management Endpoints**
```
GET /leads/sessions
- Description: Get all sessions
- Response: { "sessions": [...] }

GET /leads/{session_id}
- Description: Get leads for specific session
- Response: { "leads": [...] }

POST /leads/update-nationality
- Description: Update lead nationality
- Parameters: { "username": "user1", "nationality": "TÜRK" }
```

D. Frontend Component Architecture

**D.1 Component Hierarchy**
```
App.tsx
├── AuthContext
├── Sidebar.tsx
│   ├── Logo
│   ├── Menu Items
│   └── User Info
├── Dashboard.tsx
├── Campaigns.tsx
│   ├── Profile Scraping Tab
│   ├── Nationality Classification Tab
│   ├── Message Campaign Tab
│   └── Results Display
├── Leads.tsx
│   ├── Session Filter
│   ├── Data Table
│   └── Export Options
├── Accounts.tsx
│   ├── Account Management
│   └── Status Display
└── Messages.tsx
    └── Single Message Sender
```

**D.2 State Management**
```typescript
interface AppState {
  isAuthenticated: boolean;
  user: User | null;
  leads: Lead[];
  sessions: Session[];
  instagramAccounts: InstagramAccount[];
  loading: boolean;
  error: string | null;
}
```

E. Security Implementation

**E.1 Authentication Flow**
```
User Login → Credential Validation → JWT Token Generation → Token Storage
     ↓              ↓                      ↓                ↓
Frontend → Backend API → Database Check → Token Response → Local Storage
     ↓              ↓                      ↓                ↓
Protected Routes → Token Verification → User Authorization → Access Control
```

**E.2 Security Measures**
- **API Key Management:** Environment variables for sensitive keys
- **Input Validation:** Comprehensive data validation and sanitization
- **Rate Limiting:** Request throttling to prevent abuse
- **CORS Configuration:** Cross-origin request security
- **SQL Injection Prevention:** Parameterized queries
- **XSS Protection:** Input sanitization and output encoding

F. Performance Optimization Strategies

**F.1 Frontend Optimizations**
- **Code Splitting:** Lazy loading of components
- **Bundle Optimization:** Webpack optimization
- **Caching:** Browser caching strategies
- **Virtual Scrolling:** Large dataset rendering
- **Memoization:** React.memo for component optimization

**F.2 Backend Optimizations**
- **Database Indexing:** Optimized query performance
- **Connection Pooling:** Efficient database connections
- **Caching:** Redis caching for frequent queries
- **Async Processing:** Non-blocking operations
- **Resource Management:** Memory and CPU optimization

**F.3 Performance Metrics**
```
Response Time Targets:
- API Endpoints: < 200ms
- Database Queries: < 100ms
- Page Load: < 2s
- Component Render: < 50ms

Throughput Targets:
- Concurrent Users: 100+
- API Requests: 1000/min
- Database Operations: 5000/min
- File Processing: 100MB/min
```

H. Deployment Configuration

**H.1 Docker Configuration**
```dockerfile
# Backend Dockerfile
FROM python:3.11-slim
WORKDIR /app
COPY requirements.txt .
RUN pip install -r requirements.txt
COPY . .
CMD ["uvicorn", "api:app", "--host", "0.0.0.0", "--port", "8000"]

# Frontend Dockerfile
FROM node:18-alpine
WORKDIR /app
COPY package*.json ./
RUN npm install
COPY . .
RUN npm run build
CMD ["npm", "start"]
```

**H.2 Environment Configuration**
```yaml
# docker-compose.yml
version: '3.8'
services:
  backend:
    build: .
    ports:
      - "8000:8000"
    environment:
      - SUPABASE_URL=${SUPABASE_URL}
      - SUPABASE_KEY=${SUPABASE_KEY}
  
  frontend:
    build: ./frontend
    ports:
      - "3000:3000"
    depends_on:
      - backend
  
  nginx:
    image: nginx:alpine
    ports:
      - "80:80"
    volumes:
      - ./nginx.conf:/etc/nginx/nginx.conf
```

**I.1 Logging Structure**
```python
Log Levels:
- DEBUG: Detailed information for debugging
- INFO: General information about system operation
- WARNING: Warning messages for potential issues
- ERROR: Error messages for failed operations
- CRITICAL: Critical errors that require immediate attention

Log Categories:
- SYSTEM: System-level operations and health
- USER_ACTION: User interactions and activities
- API_REQUEST: API calls and responses
- DATABASE: Database operations and queries
- SCRAPING: Web scraping activities
- CLASSIFICATION: AI classification processes
- CAMPAIGN: Message campaign operations
- PERFORMANCE: Performance metrics and monitoring
- SECURITY: Security events and threats
```
